> Cikakken bayani kan kowace environment variable da OmniRoute ke ganewa.
> Don samfurin farawa cikin sauri, duba [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Dole ne kowace variable da aka rubuta bayaninta a nan ta bayyana a cikin `.env.example`, haka kuma
> kowace variable da ke cikin `.env.example` dole ne ta bayyana a nan. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> yana tabbatar da bin wannan ƙa'ida yayin commit da kuma a cikin CI. Don tsallake wata variable da gangan, ƙara ta a
> allowlist da ke cikin `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Sirrin da Ake Buƙata

**Dole ne** a saita waɗannan kafin fara gudanarwa karo na farko. Idan babu su, application ɗin zai ƙi farawa ko kuma ya yi aiki da saitunan da ba su da tsaro.

| Maɓalli                      | Ana buƙata        | Tsoho                           | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **E**             | _(babu)_                        | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Yana sanya hannu/tabbatar da duk kukis ɗin zaman dashboard (JWT). Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **E**             | _(babu)_                        | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Maɓallin ɓoyewar AES don ƙimomin maɓallin API da aka adana a SQLite. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | **E**             | `CHANGEME`                      | Rubutun bootstrap                                         | Yana saita kalmar sirrin farko ta dashboard ɗin mai gudanarwa (ta yi daidai da tsohuwar ƙimar `.env.example` — an bar ta a bayyane cewa ba ta da tsaro domin tilasta sauyawa). **Sauya kafin amfani na farko.** Bayan shiga, sauya ta ta Dashboard → Settings → Security.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **E** (a samarwa) | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Sirrin da aka raba don gadar WebSocket ta Codex Responses ta ciki. Yana tantance buƙatun gada tsakanin mai isar da WS na Electron/browser da OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ANA BUƘATARSA a samarwa — idan ba a saita shi ba, za a ƙi duk buƙatun gadar WS.** Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | A'a               | _(git SHA)_                     | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Takamaiman id na karya-cache na service-worker don ƙwarangwal ɗin PWA (#11779); shi ne na farko a jerin tantancewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | A'a               | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Na biyu a jerin — masu gina PaaS (misali irin na Heroku) ne ke saita shi a matsayin commit ɗin da aka tura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | A'a               | _(an samo)_                     | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Ƙimar jama'a ta lokacin gini da client ke amfani da ita wajen yin rijistar `/sw.js?v=…`; ana samo ta daga biyun da ke sama, sannan git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | A'a (ta atomatik) | _(ta atomatik a kowane farawa)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Sirrin kowane process da ke tabbatar da cewa hatimin peer-IP da aka amince da shi ya fito ne daga uwar garken HTTP na OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware na authz yana amincewa da kasancewar buƙata ta gida (takaita hanyoyin LOCAL_ONLY ga loopback/LAN) ne kawai idan hatimin yana ɗauke da wannan token. Ana ƙirƙirarsa ta atomatik a kowane farawa — a bar shi ba a saita ba; a kafa shi kawai don saitunan process da yawa waɗanda dole ne su raba hatimin. |

### Umarnin Ƙirƙirawa

```bash
# Ƙirƙiri dukkan sirrin huɗu lokaci guda:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!CAUTION]
> Kada a taɓa commit na fayilolin `.env` masu ɗauke da ainihin sirrika zuwa version control. `.gitignore` ya riga ya ware `.env`, amma a tabbatar kafin turawa.

---

## 2. Ma’aji & Ma’adanar Bayanai

OmniRoute yana amfani da **SQLite** (ta hanyar `better-sqlite3`) don duk adanawa na dindindin. Waɗannan sauye-sauye suna sarrafa wurin bayanai, ɓoyewa, da zagayowar rayuwa.

| Sauyi                                      | Tsoho                           | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`                 | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Babban kundin adireshi na DB na SQLite, kwafin ajiya, da fayilolin bayanai. Sauya shi don kundin Docker ko hanyoyi na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Hanyar kaucewa kariyar DATA_DIR ta gwaji/kimantawa (#10428). Ana karkatar da gwaje-gwaje da binciken eval/print na Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, har da siffofin `--eval=`/`--print=`) waɗanda ba su da `DATA_DIR` zuwa kundin wucin gadi da za a jefar domin kada su iya buɗe ainihin ma’adanar bayanan mai gudanarwa; saita zuwa `1` don komawa amfani da ainihin kundin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA na artifact da ke gudana. `npm run build:release` ne ke sanya shi; ana iya shigar da shi cikin containers da ake jigila ba tare da alamar `dist/BUILD_SHA` ba. Ana nuna shi a matsayin `system.buildSha` a `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                   | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref ɗin da ƙofar tabbatar da asalin pack-artifact ke kwatanta build SHA da shi (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Saita zuwa `1` don ba da damar shirya build wanda SHA ɗinsa ba ya kan layin release, tare da yin rijistarsa a matsayin canary da aka yi da gangan maimakon ƙofar ta gaza (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Maɓallin API na binciken smoke na tura canary, wanda ake aikawa a matsayin `Authorization: Bearer` zuwa `/v1/chat/completions`. Rubutun tura tsarin ne kawai ke amfani da shi (#10429), uwar garken ba ta taɓa amfani da shi. Ba shi da alaƙa da sauye-sauyen `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` na tsarin gwajin smoke na CLI da ake shiga da gangan (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` a cikin `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — duba [Haɗe-haɗen CLI → Binciken smoke na ainihi](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Alamar matakin build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` yana saita ta zuwa `1`, kuma kowane build worker da aka ƙaddamar yana gajarta ta, domin layin DB ya mayar da stub marar aiki maimakon loda native addon na better-sqlite3 (wanda ke dakatar da worker yayin fita). Ba a taɓa saita ta ga uwar garken da ke gudana ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                             | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken prebuild na zaɓi na native dependency ga vendored trees na musamman. Wannan ba ya sa dependency da ta ɓace ta zama mai yiwuwa a build; yi amfani da shi kawai idan ana samar da native dependencies ta wata hanya dabam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Laƙabin madadin** `DATA_DIR`, wanda ake dubawa kawai idan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` ba. Ana amfani da shi don gano cache ɗin zaman thread-sticky na mai aiwatar da PromptQL da ke kan faifai (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); idan ba a saita ko ɗaya daga cikin sauye-sauyen ba, cache ɗin zai kasance a ƙwaƙwalwa kawai (ba za a adana shi bayan sake farawa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Kundin da **runtime plugin scanner** ke karantawa — kuma tushen da plugin manager ke girkawa a ciki — yana maye gurbin tsohon wurin da aka samo daga home (#11827). Nuna shi zuwa plugin tree da aka bind-mount a Docker/K8s maimakon matsar da HOME kawai don sauya wurin hanyar bincike (HOME yana sarrafa duk sauran halayen da suka danganci home ma). Ba a saita ba = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ko `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` idan process ɗin bai fitar da home ko kaɗan ba — wannan sauyin yana kawar da rashin ganowa ba tare da sanarwa ba. Ana rubuta kundin da aka tantance sau ɗaya yayin farawa a matsayin `scanner.dir_resolved` tare da input ɗin da ya yi nasara. Na gefen uwar garke kawai: plugins na umarnin CLI suna ci gaba da amfani da nasu `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` (sashe na 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(babu komai = an kashe)_       | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Maɓallin AES don rufaffen cikakken ma'ajiyar bayanan SQLite yayin da take ajiye. Samar da shi da `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                            | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Alamar sigar maɓallin ɓoyewa. Ƙara ta yayin sauya maɓalli domin tallafa wa warware ɓoyewar tsofaffin kwafin ajiya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                         | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Idan `true`, ana tsallake kwafin ajiyar fayil ɗin SQLite na yau da kullum/kafin rubutawa (adana/share farashin models.dev, rubuta saituna). Kwafin ajiya na hannu da na kafin maidowa za su ci gaba da aiki. Wannan **ba ya** kashe tilas ɗin hoton kariya mai ɗorewa na mai gudanar da ƙaura ko kariyar ƙaura mai yawa ga DB mai ɗorewa da ke akwai. Ana iyakance kwafin ajiyar da ba na hannu ba zuwa mafi yawa sau ɗaya cikin mintuna 60. **Settings → Storage** na dashboard na iya kashe kwafin ajiya na yau da kullum kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon madadi** na `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Ana karɓarsa a matsayin madadin idan babban canjin ba ya nan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Tsohon madadi** (nau'in da aka ɓoye da Base64) da ake karɓa a matsayin madadin. Ana warware shi ta atomatik kafin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Sauya tazarar binciken lafiyar SQLite na lokaci-lokaci (ms). Idan ba a saita ba, ana samo tsoffin ƙimomi daga `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(an cire)_                     | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **An cire.** Gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` kai tsaye na lokaci-lokaci na iya lalata taswirar wal-index da ake rabawa kuma ya sa tsarin ya faɗi da SIGBUS (#13973), saboda haka jadawalin ba ya wanzuwa. Canjin ba ya aiki: ƙima mai kyau tana rubuta gargadin daina amfani sau ɗaya, yayin da `0` ko rashin saitawa ba sa fitar da komai. Ana kula da WAL ta wuraren dubawa na PASSIVE (a ƙasa), sannan wurin dubawa na lokacin rufewa yana rage shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (minti 5)              | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Sauya tazarar `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` mai yawan gudana (ms). Yana rage firamomin WAL da ke jiran aiki domin wuraren dubawa su kasance masu sauri, kuma fayil ɗin WAL ya kasance cikin iyaka tsakanin ragewar lokacin rufewa. `0` yana kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                           | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Idan zagayen PASSIVE ya tarar da fayil ɗin WAL ya zarce wannan girman, a gudanar da `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` domin WAL ya fara daga farko ba tare da sake rubuta wal-index da aka tsara ba. An cire wuraren dubawa na yanayin truncate masu aiki kai tsaye (duba layin `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                         | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Saita zuwa `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` domin fita daga tsarin bayan matsin albarkatu mai tsanani ya ci gaba har tsawon `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, ta yadda mai sa ido (systemd `Restart=always`, dokar sake farawa ta Docker) zai dawo da tsari mai tsabta maimakon ci gaba da ba da 503 har abada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (minti 2)              | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tsawon lokacin da matsin mai tsanani dole ne ya ci gaba kafin fitowar sake-fara-kai ta gudana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_          | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Takamaiman hanyar fayil (cikakkiya ko dangane da cwd) zuwa `sql-wasm.wasm` lokacin amfani da adaftan madadin WASM na `sql.js`. Ana gano ta atomatik ta hanyar dogaro na fakiti da tsarin fayiloli masu yiwuwa idan ba a saita ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                            | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Adadin kwanakin da wuraren dubawa, fayilolin shigarwa/fitarwa/kuskure da aka ambata, da layin aikin Batch API na ƙarshe (an kammala/ya gaza/an soke/ya ƙare) za su kasance ajiye kafin sharewa ta zagayen tsaftacewa na atomatik. Yana fara aiki ne kawai bayan an kunna `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ya yi daidai da lokacin riƙe sakamakon Batch API na OpenAI. Ba ya shafar hanyar `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` da mai gudanarwa ke kunnawa, wadda ke ci gaba da aiki ba tare da sharaɗi ba (babu tacewa ta shekaru) bisa ƙira.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                         | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Idan `true`, a bar zagayen tsaftacewa na atomatik ya share ayyukan Batch API na ƙarshe (da wuraren dubawarsu) da suka wuce `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, sannan ya goge abun BLOB na fayilolin da aka loda bayan sun wuce `expires_at` nasu. A kashe yake ta asali: kowace shigarwa da ke akwai za ta riƙe wannan bayanin daidai yadda yake a baya har sai mai gudanarwa ya zaɓi shiga. Haka kuma tutar fasali ce da za a iya gyarawa a dashboard — duba `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Lokacin aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake duba lafiyar DB gaba ɗaya yayin farawa. Yana da amfani ga ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci da gwaje-gwajen haɗawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                             | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` don tilasta kunna madaukin duba lafiyar DB, ko da kuwa a yanayi na yau da kullum za a tsallake shi (misali, ayyuka masu ɗan gajeren lokaci).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                             | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake ɗumama native-runtime yayin `npm install`. Yana da amfani a girkawa ta CI/headless inda aka riga aka gina sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Sauya kundin adireshin da mai gudanar da ƙaura yake bincikawa. Yana da amfani yayin tura ƙaura da aka haɗa cikin gine-gine na musamman.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ƙarin kundin adiresoshin ƙaura a matsayin shigarwar `namespace=dir` waɗanda aka raba da alamar raba hanya ta dandali (misali `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). Ana rubuta fayilolin da aka samu a wurin a matsayin `<namespace>-<number>`, don haka rarrabawa da ke tura ƙaurarta ba za ta taɓa karo da guraben lambobi na upstream ba. Shigarwa mara tsari, namespace mara inganci, ko kundin adireshi da babu shi zai haifar da kuskure yayin farawa maimakon tsallake schema a ɓoye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                            | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Matsakaicin kariya daga tarin ƙaura masu jiran aiwatarwa (#3416). Farawa zai tsaya idan yawan ƙauran da ke jira a DB da ke akwai ya zarce wannan adadi (yana karewa daga teburin bin diddigi da aka goge). Ƙara shi don dawo da tsohon madadin bayanai; saita zuwa `0` don kashe wannan binciken.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_   | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Kundin aikin ƙofar sakin `check:install-upgrade`. Yana buƙatar kusan 12 GB (bishiyoyin girkawa biyu na kusan ~3 GB tare da tarball), don haka bai kamata ya gudana a kan ƙaramin tmpfs ba — a kan self-hosted runner ɗin, `/tmp` tmpfs ne mai goyon bayan RAM na 12 GB, kuma ƙofar ta cika shi, ta yanke kunshin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(tsohuwar ƙima a cikin lamba)_ | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Tazarar flush (ms) ga mai rubuta kashe kuɗi/farashi na rukuni-rukuni. Ƙananan ƙimomi suna rage haɗa rubuce-rubuce; manyan ƙimomi suna rage cunkoson DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(tsohuwar ƙima a cikin lamba)_ | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar kashe kuɗi da za a ajiye a buffer kafin tilasta flush. Ƙara shi a turawa masu babban QPS; rage shi idan iyakantaccen ƙwaƙwalwar ajiya ya fi muhimmanci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Saita zuwa `"true"` don fitar da rajistan debug na `[ProxyFetch]` a kan hanyar relay ta Vercel. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa don guje wa fallasa alamun routing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                         | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Saita zuwa `"true"` ko `"1"` don haɗa IP na abokin hulɗa/egress da prefix na asusu a cikin layin rajistan tsari mai cikakken bayani na `[ProxyEgress]`. Ana barinsa a KASHE ta tsohuwa domin rajistan tsarin kada ya fallasa IP ko prefix na asusu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Saita zuwa `1` don buga bayanan binciken lokacin kowace buƙata (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) daga umarnin quota na CLI zuwa stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(ta atomatik)_                 | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Takamaiman hanya da duba lafiyar container ke gwadawa. Idan ba a saita ba, gwajin zai samo ta daga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; saita ta yana sake kunna endpoint na zurfin sa-ido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(ba a saita ba)_               | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Saita zuwa kowace ƙima marar komai don fitar da bayanan binciken `[omniroute completion]` daga hanyoyin cache na kammala umarnin shell na CLI (karantawa/sabuntawa/rubutawa). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — waɗannan cache suna gaza a ɓoye domin cache da ya ɓace/ya lalace kada ya taɓa karya kammalawa da tab.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin lokacin sake gwadawa ga kowane abu na rukuni (ms). Abubuwan da suka wuce wannan lokaci ana yi musu alamar gazawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                          | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Jinkirin asali (ms) don jan-baya na exponential a sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                  | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Iyakar (ms) don jan-baya na exponential tsakanin sake gwada abubuwan batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                             | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Matsakaicin adadin batch da ake sarrafawa a lokaci guda. Ƙara shi don haɓaka yawan aiki; bar shi ƙasa don kauce wa cunkoson iyakance ƙimar buƙatu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Kafin canza wata rumbun bayanai mai ɗorewa da ke akwai, mai gudanar da ƙaura yana wallafa cikakken,
> snapshot mai adireshin abun ciki a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Wallafawa na buƙatar tsarin fayil
> wanda ke goyon bayan hard links marasa sake-rubutawa a tsarin fayil ɗaya tare da daidaitawar fayil mai ɗorewa. Haka kuma, masaukin POSIX
> na buƙatar daidaitawar kundin adireshi; a Windows, Node na iya ƙin karɓar handles na kundin adireshi, don haka OmniRoute yana flush na
> fayil ɗin da aka wallafa kuma yana ɗaukar daidaitawar shigarwar kundin adireshi a matsayin ƙoƙari gwargwadon iko.
> Idan `DATA_DIR` da aka mount ba zai iya samar da waɗannan tabbacin ba, farawa zai gaza cikin aminci kafin aiwatar da
> ƙaura. Matsar da `DATA_DIR` zuwa volume mai waɗannan primitives; kada a yi amfani da
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` don tsallake amincin ƙaura.

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayin Amfani          | Saituna                                                                          |
| ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Haɓakawa a gida**     | Bar duk tsoffin saituna. DB tana a `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                  |
| **Docker**              | `DATA_DIR=/data` + mount volume a `/data`.                                       |
| **An ɓoye yayin ajiya** | Saita `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + adana kwafen maɓallin! Rasa shi = rasa bayanai. |
| **CI/Gwaji**            | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — na wucin gadi ne, ba a buƙatar ɓoyewa.          |

---

## 3. Hanyar Sadarwa & Tashoshin Haɗi

| Sauyi                                       | Tsoho                                                        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                                      | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Babban tashar haɗi don duka UI na Dashboard da maƙarƙashiyar API (yanayin tashar haɗi guda ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                       | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Ƙaramin hanyar URL don gabatar da OmniRoute a bayan reverse proxy (yana saita `basePath` na Next.js; karkatarwar tantancewa tana la'akari da basePath). Misali `/omniroute`. A Docker ana haɗa ƙimar yayin `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); hotunan tushe da aka riga aka gina za su iya amfani da wata ƙimar lokacin aiki sau ɗaya a farkon container kafin Next.js ya fara. Saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa asalin jama'a wanda ya haɗa da wannan ƙaramin hanyar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(babu komai = tushe)_                                       | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Madubin `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` da browser ke iya gani, wanda ake saka shi kai tsaye lokacin gini domin nunin maƙarƙashiyar dashboard ya nuna `https://host/omniroute/v1` maimakon `https://host/v1`. Idan ba a saita shi ba, yana komawa ga `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`. Sake ginawa bayan canji (`basePath` na Next ana ƙayyade shi lokacin gini).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(ba a saita ba = ba za a taɓa iya saka shi cikin frame ba)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Zaɓin ba da damar saka shafukan HTML cikin iframe. Idan ba a saita ba, kowace hanya tana zuwa da `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Saita shi zuwa `vscode` don gabatar da shafukan (dashboard, shiga, takardu, shafin sauka) tare da `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` kuma ba tare da `X-Frame-Options` ba, domin VS Code Simple Browser ya iya nuna su (yanayin `dashboardOpen: "editor"` na OmniCopilot). Fuskar API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, laƙabban matakin tushe) tana riƙe da tsauraran headers ko ta wace hanya. `vscode` kaɗai ake ganewa — `1`/`true` ba sa kunna shi. Lokacin gini: sake ginawa bayan canji (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` don hotuna; saita shi a kan girkin da aka riga aka gina ba shi da tasiri). |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita shi, yana gabatar da proxy API na `/v1/*` a wannan tashar haɗi ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adireshin ɗaurewa na tashar haɗin API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Idan an saita shi, yana gabatar da UI na Dashboard a wannan tashar haɗi ta daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(an kashe)_                                             | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Yana iyakance haɗin tattaunawa masu gudana a lokaci guda; buƙatun da suka wuce iyakar suna samun `503` tare da `Retry-After`. Cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau tana kunna kariyar; rashin saita shi/`0` yana kashe ta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Tsayayyen ID na musamman ga wannan gateway yayin haɗa instances na OmniRoute a jere. Yana kunna binciken peer-loop mai shigowa. Haruffan da aka yarda da su: haruffa, lambobi, `.`, `_`, `:`, da `-`; iyakar haruffa 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | Jerin tushen URLs na OmniRoute da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za su iya karɓar `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. URLs na upstream da aka amince da su kai tsaye kawai ke karɓar metadata na peer; duk sauran masu samarwa ba a taɓa su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                                          | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Matsakaicin adadin instances na OmniRoute da aka ziyarta a baya waɗanda za a karɓa a kan buƙata mai sarƙaƙƙiya (`1`-`32`). Instances da aka maimaita ko kasafin hops da ya ƙare suna mayar da HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                                      | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port ɗin da aka wallafa a ɓangaren host don Dashboard a yanayin samarwa na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                                      | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port ɗin da aka wallafa a ɓangaren host don API a yanayin samarwa na Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Yana da fifiko a kan `PORT` lokacin da ake aiki a cikin Electron ko wasu wrappers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                                      | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port don sabar sa ido kai tsaye ta WebSocket a ainihin lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                  | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adireshin bind don sabar WebSocket kai tsaye. Saita zuwa `0.0.0.0` don bayyana ta a LAN (sannan kuma saita `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ƙarin origins da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka yarda su buɗe WebSocket kai tsaye. An riga an ba da izini ga origins na dashboard na loopback ta tsohuwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Ƙarin hostnames da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda aka yarda da su don origins na WebSocket kai tsaye. Ba kamar `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (cikakkun URLs na origin) ba, yana daidaita ɓangaren host kawai — yana da amfani ga saitunan LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL na jama'a don WebSocket na dashboard kai tsaye (a ɓangaren browser). Saita shi lokacin da aka sanya reverse proxy ko Cloudflare Tunnel a gaban sabar WS (misali `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); browser zai haɗu a can maimakon `ws://hostname:20132`. Hakanan ana amfani da ɓangaren pathname a matsayin hanyar upgrade ta WebSocket (tsoho: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                                       | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` da `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Saita zuwa `0` ko `false` don kashe sabar WebSocket ta ainihin lokaci (ana kunna ta ta tsohuwa, an ɗaure ta da loopback). Maɓallin CI/harness ne da ke kashe standalone helper script na WebSocket kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                                         | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Iyakar adadin buƙatun relay ga kowane (token, IP), buƙatu a minti. Ana adanawa a ƙwaƙwalwa, ga kowane instance. `0` ko ƙasa da sifili yana kashe gate na ma'aunin IP (iyakar DB ta kowane token tana ci gaba da aiki).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                                 | Jigon Next.js                                                            | Yana sarrafa yawan bayanan log, caching, bayyana cikakkun bayanan kurakurai, da ingantawar Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — tsohuwar ƙimar lamba)                       | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack shi ne bundler na asali don `npm run dev` da `npm run build` (ginawa mai sauri sau 2-3, bisa gwajin ma'auni). Saita zuwa `0` don komawa webpack a kan Windows, idan aka ci karo da rashin dacewar native binding / bundler, **ko a kan na'urori masu ƙarancin RAM** — an san cewa ginawar production ta Turbopack a wannan jerin sigar Next.js (16.2.x) tana kaiwa kololuwar amfani da ƙwaƙwalwa mafi yawa sosai fiye da webpack a kan manyan jadawalin module (gyaran fitar da ƙwaƙwalwar Turbopack na Next 16.3 bai zama tsayayye ba tukuna); komawa webpack yana kaiwa kololuwa mafi ƙasa sosai. Duba #6409.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don tsallake binciken lafiyar mutuncin SQLite lokacin farawa. Yana da amfani don saurin farawa a kan manyan rumbunan bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | systemd (ka'idar sd_notify)                                              | systemd ne ke saita shi lokacin da tsarin ke gudana ƙarƙashin service unit mai haɗin sd_notify; OmniRoute yana karanta shi (duba `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) don aika sanarwar READY/WATCHDOG. Bai kamata mai amfani ya taɓa saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                            | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Saita zuwa `1` don kashe systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ko da ana gudana ƙarƙashin systemd unit. Notifier ɗin ba ya yin komai a wajen systemd ko ta yaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `300000`                                                     | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Tazarar lokaci (ms) ga mai tsara binciken lafiyar bayanan shaida da ke gudana a bango. Mafi ƙaranci: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                                     | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) ga matsayin lafiyar bayanan shaida da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                                      | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Saita zuwa `1` ko `true` don kashe gwajin haɗin masu bayarwa na lokaci-lokaci da ke gudana a bango. Ana cire masu bayar da bincike (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS a cikin `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, misali `tavily-search`) koyaushe daga aikin binciken — "tabbatarwar" su ainihin upstream query ce da ake cajin kuɗi, don haka ba a taɓa bincika lafiyarsu bisa agogon lokaci (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                                    | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adireshin bind na sabar dev/start ta Next.js.
