Yana maye gurbin tsohuwar ƙimar `0.0.0.0` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                                  | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adireshin bind da Playwright runner ke amfani da shi lokacin ƙaddamar da Next.js. Tsohuwar ƙimarsa ita ce `127.0.0.1` don gwaje-gwajen da aka ware daga muhallinsu. **Kada a yi amfani da shi don `omniroute serve`** — yi amfani da `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` maimakon haka (POSIX shells suna saita `HOSTNAME` ta atomatik zuwa sunan na'urar; `.env` ba zai iya maye gurbinsa ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                                    | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adireshin bind don `omniroute serve`. Yana kauce wa karo da variable na POSIX shell mai suna `HOSTNAME` (wanda bash/zsh ke saitawa koyaushe zuwa sunan na'urar). Yana komawa `0.0.0.0` idan ba a saita shi ba. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Yanayin Port

```
┌─────────────────────────── Tasha Guda (tsoho) ─────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Allon sarrafawa: http://localhost:20128                                  │
│  → API:             http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions              │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Rarrababbun Tashoshi ───────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Allon sarrafawa: http://localhost:20128                                  │
│  → API:             http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions               │
│  Amfani: Bayyana API ga LAN yayin taƙaita Allon sarrafawa ga localhost.     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Samarwar Docker ────────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Yana haɗa tashoshin container da tashoshin host a docker-compose.prod.yml.│
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Tsaro & Tabbatar da Shaida

| Variable                                    | Tsoho                                                                                                                 | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                                                 | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Salt da ake haɗawa da masu gano kayan masarufi domin samar da sawun na’ura. Canza shi ga kowane turawa domin warewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(ba a saita ba = salt na bazuwar da ake ƙirƙira ga kowane shigarwa kuma a adana a `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | HMAC salt don samar da alamar tantancewar CLI ta gida. Saita wannan ƙimar yana sauya dukkan alamomin CLI da ke kan na’urar, kuma koyaushe yana da fifiko a kan salt ɗin da aka adana. Duba `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Yana saita tutar `Secure` a kan kukis na zama. **Dole ne ya zama `true`** lokacin aiki a bayan HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                                               | API middleware                                           | Idan `true`, dole ne duk buƙatun wakili na `/v1/*` su ƙunshi ingantaccen maɓallin API. **Wannan tutar ba ta ƙuntata `GET /v1/models`**, wanda maimakon haka yana bin tsarin shiga na dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) — saboda haka, samun `401` daga `/v1/models` BA ya nufin cewa inference yana da kariya. Duba `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Yana ba da damar bayyana cikakkun ƙimomin maɓallin API a cikin UI na Dashboard. Ana iya daidaita shi daga Dashboard Feature Flags; yana da haɗarin tsaro a kan instances da ake amfani da su tare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(fanko)_                                                                                                             | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | IDs na maɓallan API da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ba a rubuta buƙatunsu a log ba (bin ƙa’idojin GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(ba a saita ba = babu iyaka)_                                                                                        | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Kasafin buƙatu na kowace rana da ake amfani da shi a matsayin madadin ga maɓallan API waɗanda ginshiƙin `rate_limits` ɗinsu yake null. Idan ba a saita ba ko fanko ne: babu iyakar da ake ɗauka kai tsaye (#2289, #11017). `0` ma haka yake (babu iyaka). Cikakkiyar lamba mai kyau N tana ba da damar N/rana, 5N/mako, 20N/wata. Ƙimomi marasa tsari waɗanda ba fanko ba suna komawa ga tsoffin tagogin 1000/rana, 5000/mako, 20000/wata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Matsakaicin girman jikin buƙata da aka amince da shi. Yana ƙin payloads da suka wuce wannan iyaka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ainihin jikin buƙata da ya kai ko ya wuce wannan ma’auni yana ɗaukar atomic process-local heavyweight admission lease kafin a yi parsing na JSON (hanyar BYTE, ciki har da `POST /v1/responses`). Yana amfani da hanyar tserewa ta healthy-headroom iri ɗaya da structure-heavy ta [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437); har yanzu `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) suna iyakance shi domin kada [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ya sake faruwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                                                    | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri na hanyar taɗi, wanda ake tilastawa bisa adadin bytes da aka karanta yayin karɓa mai iyaka, har da buƙatun da ba su da `Content-Length`, ko suke da wanda ba daidai ba ko na yaudara; wanda ya wuce iyaka yana karɓar `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(ba a saita ba — babu iyakar adadin buƙatu)_                                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** wannan tsohuwar iyakar ADADIN buƙatu yanzu tana aiki ne kawai idan an saita ta a bayyane. Idan ba a saita ta ba (abin da yake tsoho), maimakon haka ana sarrafa karɓar manyan buƙatun taɗi da `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — kasafin BYTE da ake ƙirƙira ta atomatik daga ainihin iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar tsarin a cikin **process guda ɗaya** (V8 heap guda ɗaya). Buƙatun `/v1/responses` biyu masu cin karo, kowannensu kusan token 750k, suna rushe heap na kusan 12 GiB (#7849) — wannan gargadin **kasafin ƙwaƙwalwa** ne, ba matsakaicin iyakar samfur mai tsauri ta 2 ba. Process mai lafiya (heap yana ƙasa da shed ratio) NA IYA karɓar ƙarin dogayen `/v1/responses` masu gudana lokaci guda ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Dubban goma na dogayen abokan cinikin SSE (40–50) ya shafi heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ba “matsakaici 2” ba. Ƙara wannan ba tare da nazari ba domin “amfani da host” yana sake jawo #7849. Ninka **heaps** da **`DATA_DIR` masu zaman kansu N** (#11024); kada a taɓa amfani da `replicas>1` a kan fayil ɗin SQLite guda ɗaya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(ana ƙirƙira ta atomatik)_                                                                                           | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** maye gurbin kasafin bytes na karɓa da ake ƙirƙira ta atomatik (25% na mafi ƙanƙantar iyakar ƙwaƙwalwar V8/cgroup, a raba da ninkawar wucin-gadi 8x). Ana taƙaita ƙimomin da aka ƙirƙira da waɗanda aka saita kai tsaye zuwa 8 MiB–2 GiB. Body da ya fi kasafin da ke aiki girma yana gaza nan take da `413 body_exceeds_budget`; cunkoso tsakanin bodies da kowannensu za a iya sarrafa shi daban yana ci gaba da zama abin sake gwadawa da `503`. Dogayen abokan cinikin SSE 40–50 masu gudana lokaci guda ya shafi wannan kasafi + heap, ba matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri ta “2” ba. Karanta `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` a `/api/monitoring/health` kafin daidaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Shed ratio na matsin heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) don karɓar manyan buƙatun BYTE da STRUCTURE (#10183, #10268, #10437). Ana shed na buƙata mai nauyi da ke gudana lokaci guda bayan `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` tare da `503` mai yiwuwa a sake gwadawa ne kawai idan heap SHIMA ya kai ko ya wuce wannan ratio; idan heap yana lafiya, ana karɓar ta ta healthy-headroom maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (tsoho `1`)                                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙarin ƙarfin da aka iyakance don hanyar gaggawa ta healthy-heap (#10437) a kan **dukansu** STRUCTURE da BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, har da bodies ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Ba tare da wannan iyaka ba, kowace buƙata mai aiki sosai amma heap ɗinta yana lafiya tana tsallake tsarin karɓa ba tare da iyaka ba. Da zarar wannan adadin leases masu gudana lokaci guda ya fara aiki ta hanyar healthy-heap, ƙarin buƙatu masu aiki sosai suna komawa kan hanyar jira mai iyaka/shed ƊAYA da ake amfani da ita ƙarƙashin ainihin matsin heap. `0` yana kashe tsallakewar gaba ɗaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin saƙonni da ke rarraba buƙatar taɗi a matsayin mai nauyi ko da body ɗinta yana ƙasa da iyakar bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                                                  | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Adadin tools da ke rarraba buƙatar taɗi a matsayin mai nauyi ko da body ɗinta yana ƙasa da iyakar bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                                               | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ƙiyasin token mai taka-tsantsan bisa girman string wanda ke rarraba buƙata a matsayin mai nauyi; wannan wakilin kuɗin karɓa ne, ba tokenization na lissafin kuɗin provider ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (a kashe)                                                                                                         | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iyakar tarihin taɗi ta zaɓi. A kashe take ta tsohuwa: adadin saƙonni manufofin deployment ne, ba siffar gama-gari ta buƙata ba, kuma sanya iyaka a nan yana ƙin tattaunawa da `413` na ƙarshe kafin pipeline na compression ya iya sa a sarrafa su. Ana iyakance haɓakar heap ta `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` da shed na matsin heap. Saita ƙima mai kyau a deployments masu ƙarancin ƙwaƙwalwa waɗanda ke buƙatar iyaka mai tsauri; wanda ya wuce iyaka zai karɓi `413` mai tsari da ke nuna ana buƙatar taƙaitawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                                                    | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Matsakaicin iyaka mai tsauri ga response na upstream mara streaming da ake adana gaba ɗayansa a ƙwaƙwalwa. Bayan wannan, ana soke upstream reader kuma buƙatar tana gaza nan take maimakon ci gaba da haɓaka string marar iyaka har heap ya ƙare.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                                                 | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Matsakaicin adadin bytes na hanyar sadarwa da ake turawa daga headers na martanin tushen sama. Idan an wuce wannan kasafi, ana cire headers masu ƙarancin fifiko (misali, `x-codex-*`, `x-oai-request-id`) domin kasancewa cikin iyakokin headers na reverse-proxy da aka saba amfani da su. Saita ƙima mafi girma domin tura ƙarin metadata daga tushen sama, amma hakan zai ƙara girman headers na martani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                                     | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Tsohon jerin izinin CORS na asali guda ɗaya. Fi son `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` don sabbin turawa. CORS na abokan hulɗar API na burauza masu ketare-asali ne kawai; rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tabbatar da izini suna amfani da buƙatun asali ɗaya tare da kariyar CSRF mai alaƙa da zaman maimakon haka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                                                                     | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Jerin izinin CORS da waƙafi ya raba. Ba a aika wildcard sai an saita `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` a sarari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Mafita ta musamman don yanayin haɓakawa kawai, wadda ke maimaita kowane `Origin` na burauza. Kada a kunna ta a turawa na gama-gari ko na samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                                                | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuard.ts`                 | Toshe kiran masu samarwa da ke nufin kewayon adiresoshin IP masu zaman kansu/loopback/link-local. Kashe wannan kawai a keɓantattun muhallan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuard.ts`                 | Ba da damar URLs na masu samarwa da ke nuni zuwa cibiyoyin sadarwa masu zaman kansu/na cikin gida (localhost, 192.168.x.x, 10.x.x.x, da sauransu). **ANA BUƘATAR wannan don masu samarwa da ake masaukinsu da kai** (LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, Triton, SearXNG). Lokacin da yake `false`, dashboard yana ƙin tabbatar da URLs na cikin gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                                                | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuard.ts`                 | Ba da damar ƙara/tabbatar da masu samarwa a adiresoshin cikin gida/masu zaman kansu (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, kewayon masu zaman kansu) — an iyakance wannan ga hanyar tabbatar da mai samarwa. **Tsoho `true`** (na cikin gida da farko); saita `false` don tilasta tsauraran toshewa ga duk abin da ba na jama'a ba. Ana ci gaba da toshe endpoints na metadata na gajimare (169.254.169.254, metadata.google.internal) ko da menene. (#5066)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Ba hanyoyin `/v1/audio/*` (rubuta sauti zuwa rubutu, magana, fassara) damar amfani da node na mai samarwa mai dacewa da OpenAI wanda aka masaukinsa a wajen localhost. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — tura sauti zuwa remote host yana canza shaidar egress kuma dole ne ya zama shawara bayyananniya ta mai gudanarwa. Ana ba da damar nodes na loopback/masu zaman kansu (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) koyaushe kuma wannan ba ya shafarsu. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Ba `POST /v1/rerank` (da matakin sake-jera na loopback na injin ƙwaƙwalwa) damar amfani da node na mai samarwa mai dacewa da OpenAI wanda aka masaukinsa a wajen localhost — na'urar LAN ko takwaran Tailscale da ke gudanar da TEI, Infinity, vLLM, da sauransu. A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — turawa zuwa remote host yana canza shaidar egress kuma dole ne ya zama shawara bayyananniya ta mai gudanarwa. Ana ba da damar nodes na loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) koyaushe kuma wannan ba ya shafarsu. Dole ne nodes na nesa su kuma bi manufofin URL na waje na mai samarwa (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`); ba a taɓa tura buƙata zuwa hosts na metadata na gajimare ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Lokacin da aka kunna OIDC, kashe shiga da kalmar sirri domin masu amfani su iya tabbatar da kansu ta hanyar OIDC Single Sign-On kawai. Ana kuma karɓar alias ɗin kai tsaye `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; Dashboard Feature Flag mai wannan key ɗin yana da fifiko. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                                                               | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Alias na kai tsaye na `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Jerin Binciken Ƙarfafa Tsaro

```bash
# Mafi ƙarancin tsaron samarwa:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Yana buƙatar HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Tabbatar da duk kiran proxy
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Kada a taɓa fallasa keys a UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Iyaka ta 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Tsabtace Bayanai Masu Shigowa & Kariyar PII

OmniRoute yana samar da kariya mai matakai biyu: binciken kutse a ɓangaren buƙata da cire PII a ɓangaren amsa.

> **⚠️ Iyakoki:** Waɗannan matakan kariya gano barazana ne na _ƙiyasin iyakar ƙoƙari_, ba cikakken katangar kariya daga kutse cikin prompt ko tsarin PII DLP ba. Za su iya samar da sakamakon ƙarya na nuna barazana (kamar yi wa prompt na persona/RPG alama duk da cewa ba su da illa) da kuma kasa gano barazana (kamar leetspeak, tazara, da tsarin harsunan da ba Turanci ba). Su kaɗai ba su isa wajen bin ƙa'idoji ba. Daidaita yanayin aiki kuma ku gwada su da zirga-zirgarku kafin ku dogara da su.

### Ɓangaren Buƙata: Kariyar Kutse cikin Prompt

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                          |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Kunna binciken saƙonni masu shigowa don gano tsarin kutse cikin prompt.                                                                                                         |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`            | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Manufar kutse: `warn` = yi log kawai, `block` = ƙi buƙata da 400. Tsohon `redact` ba ya cire rubutun kutse; yi amfani da `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` don sake rubuta PII na buƙata. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Tsohon suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                                       |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`            | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Mafi ƙarancin matakin tsanani da `MODE=block` ke ƙi: `high` (tsoho), `medium`, ko `low`. Ana sa ido kawai kan tsarin matsakaicin tsanani sai an rage matakin.                   |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Tsohon suna na `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — halayya iri ɗaya.                                                                                                            |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Idan `true`, ɓoye PII a cikin buƙatu masu shigowa (ba ya dogara da yanayin kutse).                                                                                              |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`           | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Ɓoye sanannun tsarin API-key / secret-token daga bayanan buƙata/amsa. Ana kunna shi bisa zaɓi; yana kwaikwayon `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                         |

### Ɓangaren Amsa: Mai Tsabtace PII

| Sauyi                            | Tsoho    | Fayil ɗin Tushe           | Bayani                                                                           |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Bincika amsoshin LLM don gano PII da ya zubo kafin mayarwa ga abokin ciniki.     |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = ɓoye PII, `warn` = yi log kawai, `block` = watsar da amsar gaba ɗaya. |

### Mai Tsabtace Mahallin Hanyar VS Code Mai Token

| Sauyi                               | Tsoho | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------- | ----- | ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`   | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Yana cire mahallin editan da ke aiki a fakaice (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) daga buƙatun `/v1/vscode/[token]/*`, sannan ya ɓoye abubuwan da ke cikin fayiloli masu muhimmanci da aka haɗa a sarari. Amintacce ta tsohuwa; saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. |

### Yanayin Amfani

| Yanayi                    | Kanfigareshan                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Bin ƙa'idojin kamfani** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (toshe kutse + ɓoye PII na buƙata/amsa; yanayin suna zaman kansu) |
| **Sa ido kawai**          | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — yana yin log amma ba ya taɓa toshewa                                                                                             |
| **Amfani na kai**         | Bar duk a kashe — babu ƙarin nauyi                                                                                                                                                             |

---

## 6. Manufofin Kayan Aiki & Daidaita Hanya

| Sauyi                                                       | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                       | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                    | Yana sarrafa damar LLM ta amfani da kayan aiki/kiran ayyuka. `allowlist` = kayan aikin da aka jera kawai, `denylist` = duk sai waɗanda aka jera, `disabled` = babu ƙuntatawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json`     | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`        | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin JSON na ƙa’idojin sarrafa payload (gyare-gyaren upstream na kowane samfuri/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                           | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`        | Tazarar sake lodawa (ms) don sake loda fayil ɗin ƙa’idojin payload kai tsaye. Mafi ƙaranci `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                          | `open-sse/services/model.ts`               | Zaɓin da ake kunnawa: a tura ID na samfuran `claude-*` marasa prefix daga abokan cinikin Claude Code ta asusun OAuth na Claude Code maimakon buƙatar prefix na mai samarwa. Takamaiman prefix na mai samarwa har yanzu suna da fifiko. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ta maɓallin sauyawa a dashboard da ke shafin mai samar da Claude.                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                              | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`         | Iyakar ayyukan lokaci guda ga kowane samfuri don haɗe-haɗen round-robin (#9100). An tsayar da semaphore na haɗin round-robin kai tsaye a buƙatu 3 na lokaci guda ga kowane samfuri ba tare da damar sauyawa ba, wanda ke jera zirga-zirgar da ke da yawan ayyuka lokaci guda a bayan wannan iyaka. Ana tabbatar da shi zuwa `>= 1`, kuma a taƙaita shi zuwa `<= 32`.                                                                                                  |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                          | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`            | Zaɓi mai haɗari wanda ke tsallake binciken OmniRoute na gida kan context-window / matsakaicin token na shigarwa don buƙatun samfuri guda kai tsaye. Masu samar da upstream har yanzu suna tilasta ainihin iyakokinsu; matse prompt da iyakar token na fitarwa ta samfurin kansa suna ci gaba da aiki. Tsarin fifikon da ake amfani da shi shi ne sauyin Feature Flags DB > environment variable > tsoho; ba a buƙatar sake farawa.                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`     | Takardar YAML ta `providers:` a cikin layi (RIC-738, D4). Idan an saita (tare da ko ba tare da ɓangaren `strategy:` ba), `/v1/chat/completions` yana karkata zuwa shigarwar OpenAI-compatible mai haɗaka da aka karɓi bakuncinta da kai maimakon cloud pipeline. Idan ba a saita ba (tsoho): hanyar tana wucewa kai tsaye zuwa cloud pipeline da ake da shi. Duba `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`     | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin YAML da ke ɗauke da takardar `providers:` iri ɗaya da `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, don deployment da suka fi son fayil maimakon inline env var. Kowanne daga cikinsu yana kunna shigarwar da aka karɓi bakuncinta da kai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba — hanya a buɗe)_ | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`     | Maɓallin API na bai ɗaya na zaɓi don shigarwar da aka karɓi bakuncinta da kai mai haɗaka (D5 scaffold, an tanada shi don tsarin quota na kowane maɓalli). Idan an saita, dole ne buƙatu su ƙunshi `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Idan ba a saita ba: hanyar tana buɗe, daidai da tsarin local-provider da aka karɓi bakuncinsa da kai da ake da shi (deployment na loopback/amintacciyar hanyar sadarwa).                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`   | Takardar YAML ta `strategy:` a cikin layi don injin daidaita hanya mai tabbataccen sakamako (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, cooldown breaker, fifikon farashi, la’akari da latency, jerin fallback. Tana rinjayar ɓangaren `strategy:` na cikin layi da aka saka a cikin `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, ga kowane maɓalli. Duba `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`   | Hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin YAML da ke ɗauke da takardar `strategy:` iri ɗaya da `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, don deployment da suka fi son fayil maimakon inline env var.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don dakatar da tattara tarihin tattaunawa. `resolveConversationId()` yana mayar da sakamakon da ba a bibiyarsa kafin ya karanta SQLite ko ya tantance tarihin saƙonni, kuma wannan ya haɗa da ID na zaman da abokin ciniki ya bayar. Gudanar da zaman routing bai canza ba, kuma ba a share bayanan da ake da su. Wannan ya dace da deployments waɗanda ba sa amfani da kallon tattaunawa na dashboard kuma suke son hana turn table ci gaba da girma. |

---

## 7. URLs & Daidaitawar Cloud

| Sauyi                                    | Tsoho                                           | Fayil ɗin Asali                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                               | `http://localhost:20128`                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL na gefen uwar garke wanda ayyukan daidaitawa na ciki za su yi amfani da shi wajen kiran `/api/sync/cloud`. A bar wannan a matsayin URL na loopback/container ko da ana wakiltar manhajar ga jama'a ta proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLOUD_URL`                              | _(babu komai)_                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL na endpoint ɗin cloud relay (fasalin kuɗi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                  | `12000`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Iyakar lokacin HTTP don buƙatun daidaitawar cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                | `full`                                          | Tsarin build na Webpack                          | Bayanan martabar lokacin build (saita zuwa `minimal` don cire modules masu gata daga bundle gaba ɗaya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`               | _fitowar standalone ta .build/_                 | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Sauya saitin lokacin build don kundin adireshin fitowar standalone da matakin haɗa-wuri bayan build ke amfani da shi. Ba saitin lokacin gudana ba ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`            | _(babu komai)_                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Sirrin da aka raba da ake amfani da shi don tabbatar da sa hannun HMAC-SHA256 na amsoshin Daidaitawar Cloud.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`           | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Saita zuwa `true` don ba endpoint ɗin Daidaitawar Cloud damar sake rubuta bayanan tantancewa na gida. Tsohon ƙima shi ne `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE` | `false`                                         | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Saita zuwa `true` don ƙin amsar Daidaitawar Cloud marar sa hannu idan babu wani sirri na gida da aka saita (#13679). Duk sa hannun da yake akwai ana tabbatar da shi koyaushe — kuma koyaushe ana ƙin sa idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` ba — ba tare da la'akari da wannan flag ba. A v3.9, tsohon saitin zai koma tilastawa.                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`   | `false`                                         | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Saita zuwa `true` don komawa ga halayyar mataki ɗaya ta v3.8.5 ta "shigo da komai" ba tare da tabbatarwar mai amfani ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                   | `http://localhost:20128`                        | OAuth, Dashboard, daidaitawa                     | URL na jama'a don OAuth redirect_uri, hanyoyin haɗin Dashboard, da URLs na jama'a da aka samar. Saita wannan zuwa tsayayyen URL na jama'a idan callbacks na OAuth ko hanyoyin haɗin burauza da aka samar dole ne su yi amfani da canonical reverse-proxy host.                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                  | _(babu komai)_                                  | Gefen abokin ciniki                              | Kwafin `CLOUD_URL` na gefen abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Tsohon madadin `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | Mai warware asalin jama'a, URLs na hotuna        | Asalin OmniRoute da burauza ke gani mai fifiko mafi girma, wanda ake amfani da shi wajen samar da URLs na jama'a da tabbatar da asalin burauza wanda ba na dashboard ba. Saita wannan idan OpenWebUI ko wani relay yana isa OmniRoute ta URL na ciki amma dole ne burauzar mai amfani ta ɗauko kafofin da aka samar daga LAN, tunnel, ko asalin jama'a. **Kada** a haɗa `/v1`.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Cikakken URL na manifest ɗin plugin na provider da ake sanar da abokan cinikin sidecar. Idan ba a saita ba, OmniRoute yana samo `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` daga asalin buƙata ko HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`              | `http`                                          | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protocol da ake amfani da shi yayin samo URL na manifest ɗin plugin na provider daga HOST/PORT ba tare da asalin buƙata ba. Saita shi zuwa `https` a bayan proxy na jama'a da ke ƙare TLS idan ba a saita takamaiman `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` ba.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Yanayin amincewa na zaɓi don headers na asalin jama'a da aka tura. Ba a saita ba = kada a amince da `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` wajen yanke shawarar tsaro. `true` / `loopback` yana amincewa da host/proto da aka tura kawai daga proxy na loopback mai alamar token. `private` / `lan` kuma yana amincewa da abokan proxy na LAN mai zaman kansa. A production, fi son takamaiman `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.           |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL na callback na jama'a don ayyukan kie.ai masu gudana ba tare da jira ba. Shi ne override mai fifiko mafi girma kafin `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` da `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Wata hanyar rubuta `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. Ana komawa gare shi idan ba a saita babban variable ɗin ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Asalin jama'a da ake amfani da shi wajen haɗa URLs na callback masu gudana ba tare da jira ba. Shi ne madadin mafi ƙarancin fifiko don callbacks na kie.ai; ana kuma amfani da shi a matsayin URL na jama'a na gama-gari don sauran relays.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`               | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                    | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint na duba adadin CrofAI da shafin Usage ke amfani da shi. Yi override don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`           | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`           | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Endpoint na hukuma na amfani da OpenCode Go mai tantancewa ta API key, wanda shafin Usage ke amfani da shi. Yi override don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`        | `true`                                          | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Ƙirƙiri headers na shaidar OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUIDs na buƙata/session) a kan buƙatun opencode-go/zen zuwa upstream waɗanda abokin ciniki bai aika ba, domin Cloudflare a hanyar fita ta VPS ya karɓe su (#6210/#5997). Yana kunne ta tsohuwa tun daga #10571; kashe shi da `false`/`0`/`no`/`off`.                                                                                    |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                    | `opencode/1.18.31`                              | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | User-Agent na tsohuwa da ake amfani da shi idan `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yana kunne kuma ba a saita override na kowane provider na `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` ba. Ana amfani da shi ne kawai ga executors na opencode. A buƙatar da ba ta da key wadda upstream ke tacewa, ƙimar da aka saita wadda ba ta ɗauke da `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` za a maye gurbinta da wannan ƙimar tsohuwa maimakon a ƙi buƙatar. |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                        | `desktop`                                       | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Ƙimar da za a saka a header na `x-opencode-client` da aka ƙirƙira lokacin da `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` yake kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                       | `global`                                        | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Ƙimar da za a yi amfani da ita ga kanun `x-opencode-project` da aka ƙirƙira lokacin da aka kunna `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`    | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Saita zuwa `off` don dakatar da daidaita jikin buƙatun OpenCode marasa maɓalli (alamar streaming da jerin kayan aiki). Har yanzu ana amfani da kanun bayanai. Ana karanta wannan a kowace buƙata, saboda haka sauyi zai fara aiki nan take.                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`   | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Sunayen kayan aiki da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a ayyana a cikin buƙatar OpenCode marar maɓalli wadda ba ta ɗauke da ko ɗaya, idan har yanzu ba a lura da wani abu ga wannan model ɗin ba. Idan babu komai, za a koma ga kayan aiki guda ɗaya na wucin gadi wanda ake gaya wa model kada ya kira. Ba fiye da shigarwa 32 ba, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; ana watsi da shigarwar da ba su inganta ba.            |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`       | `https://ollama.com/settings`                   | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL na saitunan Ollama Cloud da ake amfani da shi wajen karɓo bayanan ƙayyadadden amfani. Sauya shi don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                    | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Cookie na Ollama Cloud mai suna `__Secure-session` da ake amfani da shi wajen karɓo bayanan ƙayyadadden amfani daga shafin saituna. Mai matuƙar sirri ne; idan an saita asusu da yawa, fi son filin Dashboard na kowace haɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Madadin env var na cookie na Ollama Cloud mai suna `__Secure-session`. Mai matuƙar sirri ne; idan an saita asusu da yawa, fi son filin Dashboard na kowace haɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`          | _(ba a saita ba)_                               | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Madadin env var na cookie na Ollama Cloud mai suna `__Secure-session`, wanda ake amfani da shi kafin gajerun sunayen madadin. Mai matuƙar sirri ne; idan an saita asusu da yawa, fi son filin Dashboard na kowace haɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`       | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com` | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint na iyakokin amfani na CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro).
