Sauya shi don relays / kayan gwaji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Lokacin turawa a bayan reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), saita `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` zuwa tsayayyen URL ɗinka na jama'a (misali, `https://omniroute.example.com`) idan callbacks na OAuth ko hanyoyin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira dole ne su yi amfani da wannan hostname. Idan babu wannan, callbacks na OAuth za su iya gaza saboda redirect_uri ba zai dace ba, kuma hanyoyin jama'a da aka ƙirƙira za su iya nuna internal container origin.
>
> Bar `BASE_URL` a matsayin URL na internal loopback/container don ayyukan server-to-server. Kada a yi amfani da `Origin` na browser ko hostname na jama'a don internal self-fetches masu ɗauke da bayanan shaidar shiga.
>
> Rubuce-rubucen dashboard da aka tabbatar da izini ba sa buƙatar tsayayyen public base URL: dashboard yana aika unsafe requests na same-origin tare da CSRF token da ke daure da session. Har yanzu OmniRoute yana haɗa tabbatar da public-origin a wuri guda don browser integrations waɗanda ba na dashboard ba: ana fara amincewa da env vars na public URL da aka bayyana kai tsaye; ana watsi da ɗanyen kanun `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` sai dai idan an kunna `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` kuma an yi wa immediate proxy peer alamar token a matsayin amintacce. Kada a yi amfani da saitunan CORS don gyara buƙatun dashboard na same-origin; CORS na clients na browser masu cross-origin ne kawai.

---

## 8. Wakilin Fita

Tura kiran masu samar da LLM na sama ta hanyar wakilin HTTP ko SOCKS5 domin sarrafa zirga-zirgar fita, zaɓen hanya bisa yanki, ko ɓoye adireshin IP.

| Variable                                 | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                         | Kunna wakilin SOCKS5 don kiraye-kirayen sama. Kashe shi da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | Bangaren abokin ciniki                       | Sanin samuwar SOCKS5 a ɓangaren abokin ciniki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`           | Tutar fasalin da ake kunna ta da gangan (duba [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); saitin maye gurbi na DB na dashboard ne ke da rinjaye). Tarin wakilai da juyawar kowane asusu suna daina sake ba da memban da ya gaza kwanan nan (an ƙi gwajin TCP, ko an samu 429 ta cikinsa) na wani lokaci da ke ninka duk lokacin da gazawar ta maimaitu, har zuwa iyaka. `true` (ko `1`, `yes`) yana kunna shi.  |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Wakilin HTTP don kiraye-kirayen sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Wakilin HTTPS don kiraye-kirayen sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Wakili na gama-gari (yana goyon bayan `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                 | Yana daidaita manufa ɗaya ta URL don gano IP da gwajin fitowar wakili ke amfani da ita. Idan ba a saita ba, gwajin yana fara gwada `api64.ipify.org`, sannan `api4.ipify.org`, don kada a bayar da rahoton cewa hanyoyin IPv4-kawai sun mutu (#9694).                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(ba a saita ba)_ | Ma'aunin Node.js                             | Sunayen runduna/adiresoshin IP da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda za a kewaye wakili gare su.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`          | Matsakaicin soket-soket masu aiki lokaci guda ga kowane mai rarraba wakilin HTTP/SOCKS da aka adana. Dogayen rafukan SSE kamar Codex `/v1/responses` suna buƙatar haɗi fiye da ɗaya idan buƙatu da dama suna amfani da wakilin matakin asusu ɗaya. Ana iyakance ƙimomin da suka haura `256`.                                                                                                                |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts` | Iyakar lokacin musayar farko ta SOCKS5 (haɗawa) a ms. Ƙara shi idan buƙatu masu yawa suna bugun rundunar ƙofar sadarwar gida guda ɗaya (misali, buƙatu 100 a lokaci guda) — ainihin musayar farko na iya wuce 10s idan tarin ya cika, ko da yake ana iya isa ga wakilin, wanda in ba haka ba zai bayyana a matsayin kuskuren ƙarya na `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable`. An iyakance shi zuwa `120000`. |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`            | Idan `false` ne (tsoho), buƙatar da wakilin da aka ware mata ya kasa warwarewa ana **ƙin karɓarta (fail-closed)** maimakon komawa ga haɗin kai tsaye — wannan yana hana fallasa ainihin IP. Saita `true` don dawo da tsohuwar hanyar komawa DIRECT.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                         | Kwaikwayi sawun yatsan TLS ta amfani da wreq-js (yana kwaikwayon Chrome 124). Yana magance toshewar JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`               | Jerin masu samarwa da aka amince da su, wanda aka raba da wakafi, don sabuwar hanyar TLS ta proxy (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS kai tsaye yana ci gaba da tsohon halinsa idan ba a saita shi ba; waɗannan masu samarwa kaɗai ne ake bi ta gadar sawun yatsan Chrome-124.                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts` | Ba wa mahallin burauzar Claude Turnstile Playwright damar yin watsi da kurakuran takardar shaidar HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

### Yanayi

| Yanayi                                                          | Kanfigareshan                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 ta cikin ramin SSH**                                   | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                                         |
| **Proxy na HTTP na kamfani**                                    | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com`               |
| **Kariya daga gano sawun yatsa**                                | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — yana buƙatar `wreq-js` (an haɗa shi)                                                                    |
| **Ana sarrafa zirga-zirgar fita / babu damar kai tsaye**        | Bar `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (na asali). Buƙatu za su gaza gaba ɗaya idan proxy ba ya samuwa maimakon su zubo ta hanyar haɗin kai tsaye. |
| **Tsohon tsari / ci gaba — ba da damar komawa haɗin kai tsaye** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Yana dawo da halin kafin ƙarfafa tsaro: ana amfani da haɗin kai tsaye idan warware proxy ya gaza.               |

> **Lura (tsallake tabbatarwar NVIDIA — #3226):** Wurin ƙarshen tabbatar da maɓallin API na NVIDIA
> yana maƙalewa idan aka bi da shi ta hanyar proxy na duniya/fetch da aka yi wa facin TLS (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` da gangan yana tsallake
> facin proxy don wannan kiran tabbatarwa guda ɗaya ta amfani da `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Wannan keɓantacce ne da aka rubuta kuma aka taƙaita iyakarsa — **ba ya** shafar zirga-zirgar fita ta taɗi/amfani.
> An ɗaure iyakar tsallakewar ta `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. Haɗa Kayan Aikin CLI

Yana sarrafa yadda OmniRoute ke gano da kuma ƙaddamar da sidecars na CLI (Claude Code, Codex, da sauransu).

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho                            | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = bincika PATH na tsarin; `manual` = yi amfani da takamaiman hanyoyi kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ƙarin shigarwar PATH don gano fayilolin binary na CLI (an raba su da alamar colon).                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Sauya kundin gida don karanta saitunan CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Dole ya zama cikakkiyar hanya kuma ya kasance cikin gidan tsarin aiki — **ko kuma**, a cikin container, hanyar da aka haɗa ta bind mount (haka `/host-home` yake aiki). Duk wani abu dabam zai koma kundin gida. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ba OmniRoute damar rubuta fayilolin saitin CLI (sabunta token, bayanan session). Saita zuwa `false` domin duk wani yunƙurin rubuta saitin CLI ya gaza tare da kuskuren "an hana rubutu" a sarari.                                                                                      |

| `ZCODE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `10000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Iyakar lokacin farawa (ms) kafin a ɗauki ƙaddamar da app-server na ZCode a matsayin wanda ya gaza.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ZCODE_RPC_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `30000`                          | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Iyakar lokacin RPC na kowace buƙata (ms) don kiran app-server na ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ZCODE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `120000`                         | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Matsakaicin tsawon lokaci (ms) na zagaye ɗaya na ZCode kafin mai sa ido ya dakatar da shi saboda ƙarewar lokaci.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ZCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`          | `250`                            | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                       | Tazarar binciken lokaci-lokaci (ms) don kammalawar zagayen ZCode.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                      | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts` | Kundin gida na Hermes Agent inda OmniRoute ke karanta/rubuta saitunan Hermes CLI. Ya yi daidai da env var da mai sakawa na Hermes PowerShell yake saita wa a Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                        |

### Daidaita Bayanan Martabar CLI ta Atomatik

Waɗannan tutocin fasali na zaɓi ne kuma a kashe suke ta asali. Haka kuma, ana iya kunna ko kashe su daga
dashboard na CLI Code.

| Sauyawa                               | Tsoho   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na `~/.codex/*.config.toml` ta atomatik daga kundin bayanai na yanzu. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitunan Codex masu aiki/na asali, auth, saitunan Codex-lb, ko zaɓin mai samarwa.               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Bayan daidaita samfurin mai samarwa, yana sake rubuta fayilolin bayanan martaba na Claude Code na `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` ta atomatik daga kundin bayanai na yanzu. Yana buƙatar `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ba ya taɓa canza saitunan Claude masu aiki/na asali, auth, ko zaɓin mai samarwa. |

### Misalin Docker

```bash
# Haɗa binary na na'urar mai masauki cikin container sannan ka gaya wa OmniRoute inda suke:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` yana fara aiki ne kawai idan an yi bind-mount na path ɗin daga
host — haɗa shi da mounts kamar `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (duba
profile na `host` a cikin `docker-compose.yml`). Path da ba ya cikin home na
mai amfani da container kuma ba bind mount ba ne za a yi watsi da shi, saboda duk abin da aka rubuta
a wurin zai ɓace idan aka sake ƙirƙirar container ɗin.

Image ɗin yana aiki a matsayin `USER node`, saboda haka `/root` da ba a mount ba **ba**
ingantaccen override ba ne.

| Variable                                 | Tsoho        | Source File                         | Bayani                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(atomatik)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Tilasta gano container ya kasance a kunne (`1`/`true`) ko a kashe (`0`/`false`). Ana buƙatar wannan ne kawai a runtimes da ganowa ta atomatik ba ta gano su ba. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`      | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Ba da damar rubuta config na kayan aikin CLI zuwa path na container da ba a mount ba duk da haka. Daidaitaccen zaɓin CLI shi ne `--allow-container-write`.      |

### Masu taimakawa na CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Waɗannan variables suna daidaita halayen CLI binary na `omniroute` da kansa (ba gano sidecar
da ke sama ba).

| Variable                     | Tsoho             | Source File                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ---------------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`             | _(system)_        | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Tilasta harshen output na CLI. Locale na BCP-47 (misali, `en`, `pt-BR`). Yana maye gurbin env vars na locale na system (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`         | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don tura stdout/stderr na server zuwa terminal a supervised mode. Daidai yake da flag na `--log` a kan `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token na tabbatar da na'ura da ake saka a matsayin header na `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ana samar da shi ta atomatik a task 8.12.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Iyakar lokacin HTTP na kowane yunƙuri (ms) don buƙatun CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS` | `60000`           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Matsakaicin lokacin (ms) da CLI ke jira endpoint na health na server kafin ya nuna gargaɗin timeout. Yana da amfani ga cold starts masu jinkiri (misali, Windows). Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ta `--ready-timeout`.                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`          | `0`               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Saita zuwa `1` don nuna bayanan bincike na retry/backoff zuwa stderr yayin umarnin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`      | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory na musamman don gano plugins na CLI (packages na `omniroute-cmd-*`). Idan ba a saita ba, tsoho shi ne `~/.omniroute/plugins/`. Na CLI kawai — ba ya taɓa isa ga plugin scanner na gefen server, wanda `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` ke nuna masa hanya (sashe na 2). |

Yana maye gurbin ganowa ta atomatik ta localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Rukunin MCP/A2A                                             | Maɓallin API don kiran kayan aikin MCP na ciki da ƙwarewar A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID na maɓalli don danganta rajistar binciken MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | Na gado                                                     | Laƙabin gado na `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Adadin kwanakin da ake riƙe tarihin ayyukan A2A a ma'ajiyar bayanai ta gida kafin sharewar yau da kullum ta goge layi. Idan ba a saita ba, ba lamba ba ne, ko `<= 0`, zai koma `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Maɓallin kashe fasalin lura da nasarar gano ƙwaƙwalwar A2A. Saita zuwa `0` don tsallake binciken dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa gaba ɗaya ga wani aiki; kowace ƙima dabam (har da rashin saitawa) tana barin fasalin a kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Yana kunna ƙarshen hanyar tantance matsalolin da aka rikoda na Issue Agent ba tare da intanet ba/a gida. A bar shi a kashe sai dai idan ana gudanar da hanyoyin aikin tantance rikodi na gida a sarari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(ba a saita ba)_                                   | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) na gudu guda na tantance rikodi na Issue Agent. Ana iyakance shi zuwa matsakaicin ƙimar ciki; yana komawa ga tsohuwar ƙimar da aka gina idan ba a saita shi ba ko ba shi da inganci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(mahallin da ke aiki)_                             | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Mahalli/furofayil na yanayin nesa na CLI don umarnin `omniroute`; yana maye gurbin mahallin da ke aiki a ma'ajiyar mahallai ta gida. Daidai yake da `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Kashe zaɓin backend na OS-keychain na `keytar` don bayanan shaidar mahallin CLI. Idan an kunna, bayanan shaidar za su ci gaba da kasancewa a cikin `config.json` mai yanayin `0600`, kuma CLI zai fitar da gargaɗin komawa madadin sau ɗaya; an tanade shi don aiki marar allon sarrafawa/a cikin container da aka yi da gangan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `true`                                              | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Tilasta ikon samun dama bisa scopes a kan kiran kayan aikin MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazarar binciken iyakar ƙimar mai samarwa da ƙota.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗebo ƙotar OAuth a jere a yayin aiki tare na jimla; ana ɗebo haɗin OAuth ɗaya bayan ɗaya don guje wa turawa zuwa uwar-garke da yawa lokaci guda. `0` yana hana wannan (a lokaci guda).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) tsakanin ɗebo ƙotar uwar-garke a jere a kan hanyar dubawa kafin kowace buƙata/sa ido; yana ba kiran hanyar sadarwa masu gudana lokaci guda tazara domin kada asusu da yawa a kan IP guda su yi wa uwar-garken ambaliya. An haɗa shi da masu ɗebo ƙotar Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (wuraren ɗebo duka biyu), OpenCode, da Crof (#6009, #6911). Har yanzu ba a rufe hanyar turawa ta gama-gari `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ba — ana bin diddiginta dabam. | `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_IDLE_MS`               | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Tsawon lokacin zaman banza a cikin milliseƙondi kafin a dakatar da ma’aikacin matse bayanai da ba a amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Babban maɓallin circuit-breaker na kowane engine a cikin stacked-pipeline na T02. **Na zaɓi ne (a kashe ta tsohuwa)** — idan yana kunne, ana tsallake engine da ke yawan jefa kuskure a buƙatu daban-daban (fail-open) na wani lokacin hucewa; idan a kashe = halayyar legacy mai daidaiton byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Yawan gazawar jere a buƙatu daban-daban kafin breaker na engine ya buɗe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Adadin milliseƙondin da ake ci gaba da tsallake engine da breaker ɗinsa ya buɗe kafin gwajin half-open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Matakin retrieval-feedback na T08/H8 CCR: kowane karo na baya da aka dawo da wani tubalin da aka adana yana ƙara ingantaccen `minChars` nasa a layi madaidaici (abubuwan da ake yawan dawo da su suna raguwa wajen matsewa; dawo da su sau `>=3` = ba za a taɓa matse su ba). `1` yana kashe matakin (binary skip kawai a bakin ƙimar).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ma'ajiyar tubalan CCR mai ɗorewa (#9061). Tana tallafa wa ma'ajiyar cikin ƙwaƙwalwa da SQLite domin tubali ya ci gaba da kasancewa bayan cirewar LRU, TTL, sake farawa, ko lokacin da aikin dawo da bayanai ya sauka a wani instance. Saita `false` don ajiye tubalan a cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. Tubalan da suka wuce 512KB da runtimes na cloud za su ci gaba da kasancewa cikin ƙwaƙwalwa kawai ko da kuwa saitin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Babban maɓallin daskare prefix na T08/H5 bisa amfanin da aka lura da shi. **Sai an zaɓa (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa)** — idan yana kunne, system prompt da aka lura da shi sau `>=` adadin threshold ana ɗaukarsa a matsayin tsayayyen prefix da za a iya cache, kuma ana kiyaye shi daga compression har ma ga providers waɗanda static cache heuristic bai gano ba (daskarewa tana _kiyayewa_ kawai, ba ta taɓa canza abu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Yawan lokutan lura da system prompt kafin a ɗauke shi a matsayin daskararren tsayayyen prefix.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Bootstrap script yana saita `true` bayan saitin farko. Yana sarrafa bayyanar setup wizard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Hanyar kaucewa ta gaggawa: ba da damar request body ya sake ƙayyade project field na Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Manufofin Google One AI credits: `off` ba ya taɓa saka credits, `retry` yana saka su sau ɗaya bayan quota 429 da ya cancanta, yayin da `always` ke saka su a request na farko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Ba mai fassara request na Antigravity damar tsallake tsauraran matakan tabbatar da CLI request-signature idan upstream ya ƙi sahihan signatures (yanayin debug/tsohon-CLI). Ƙimar da ba sifili ba tana kunna wannan tsallakewar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Sake ƙayyade token-file path na Antigravity CLI (agy) don shigo da local login da aka gano ta atomatik.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Gadar OAuth CLI (Na Ciki)

| Variable            | Default           | Source File                     | Description                                    |
| ------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | gano-ta-atomatik  | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL na server don gadar auth ta CLI↔OmniRoute. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Auth token don gadar CLI.                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | User ID don sessions na gadar CLI.             |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.            |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.             |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Tsohon alias na `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.           |

## 11. Takardun Shaidar Mai Bayar da OAuth

Takardun shaidar da aka tanada a ciki don **haɓakawa a localhost**. Don turawa zuwa sabar nesa, yi rajistar naka a na’urar sarrafa masu haɓakawa ta kowane mai bayarwa.

| Sauyi                             | Mai bayarwa             | Bayanan kula                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Client na jama'a — ba a buƙatar sirri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`        | Claude Code             | Sauya redirect URI. Tsoho: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`           | Codex / OpenAI          | Client na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | Gemini (Google)         | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` mai dacewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`      | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Client na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | Antigravity (Google)    | Yana buƙatar `_SECRET` mai dacewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | GitHub Copilot          | Client na jama'a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`     | GHE Copilot             | Sauyi na zaɓi don client id na OAuth na GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Idan ba a saita shi ba, zai koma ga tsohon ƙimar jama'a ta `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`          | GitHub Copilot          | Sauyi na zaɓi don ID ɗin haɗa client na GitHub Copilot da ake aikawa a cikin headers na `Copilot-Integration-Id` da `Editor-Plugin-Version`. Tsohuwar ƙima ita ce `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | Madadin maɓallin API da `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` ke amfani da shi idan babu takardar shaidar kowace haɗi. Na zaɓi ne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                   | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Keɓaɓɓiyar hanya zuwa binary na Devin CLI (`devin`). Ana gano ta ta hanyar `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`      | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID ɗin client na OAuth don GitLab Duo. Yi rajistar app a `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` tare da redirect URI `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` da scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Zai koma ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`  | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Sirrin client na OAuth don GitLab Duo. Na zaɓi ne — tsarin PKCE ba ya buƙatar sirri. Zai koma ga `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`             | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Sauya tushen URL na GitLab (GitLab mai zaman kansa). Tsohuwar ƙima ita ce `https://gitlab.com`. Zai koma ga `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                 | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Tsohon madadin `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita nau'in `_DUO_` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`          | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Tsohon madadin `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`      | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Tsohon madadin `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` wanda `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`       | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`       | Qoder                   | Saita shi don kunna Qoder OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`           | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`        | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`           | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`     | Qoder                   | Madadin maɓallin API kai tsaye (yana tsallake OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`             | Qoder                   | ID na workspace don Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`       | Qoder                   | Wani suna na `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`            | Qoder                   | Sauya kundin saitunan Qoder CLI (zaman PAT mai zaman kansa, yana hana lalata shiga ta browser).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`    | Blackbox Web            | Token `tk` na frontend da za a aika a matsayin `validated` a `/api/chat`. Ana buƙatarsa lokacin da Blackbox ke tilasta daidaituwar token; in ba haka ba, OmniRoute zai koma amfani da UUID bazuwar. Duba matsala #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`          | Vision Bridge guardrail | Base URL mai dacewa da OpenAI don kiran vision-bridge da ba na Anthropic ba. A tsohuwa, yana amfani da tsohon OpenAI URL env ko api.openai.com. Nuna shi zuwa self-loop na OmniRoute na `/v1` ko duk wani endpoint mai dacewa da OpenAI (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Matsala #2232. Lokacin da URL ɗin yake `/v1` na OmniRoute kansa, ƙaramin buƙatar bayani yana aika `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` kuma yana tabbatar da izini ta amfani da bayanan shaidar self-loop da aka tantance (`sk_omniroute` sentinel a yanayin local, ko `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) domin turawa masu `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` su yi aiki. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`           | Vision Bridge guardrail | Maɓallin API na URL da ke sama. Yana fin ƙarfi kan OpenAI / Google env vars na kowane provider don kiran vision-bridge da ba na Anthropic ba. Samfuran Anthropic suna ci gaba da amfani da keɓantacciyar hanyar maɓallin Anthropic. Matsala #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Je zuwa [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Ƙirƙiri OAuth 2.0 Client ID (nau'i: "Web application")
> 3. Ƙara URL na server ɗinka a matsayin Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Sauya ƙimomin bayanan shaidar da ke cikin `.env`.

## 12. Sauya Saitunan User-Agent na Mai Bayarwa

Sauya kanun `User-Agent` da ake aikawa zuwa kowane mai bayarwa na sama. Ajin tushe na executor ne ke tantance wannan a lokacin aiki:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Tushe:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Sauyi | Ƙimar Tsoho | Lokacin Sabuntawa |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.258 (external, cli)` | Lokacin da Anthropic ta fitar da sabon sigar CLI |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Saita zuwa `1`/`true` don tura sunayen kayan aikin harness na ɓangare na uku kai tsaye zuwa Anthropic a duka hanyoyin da ke zuwa Anthropic (OAuth na asali da CLIProxyAPI). A tsoho, executor yana sanya madadin suna ga sunayen kayan aikin da ba na Claude Code ba ta hanya mai tabbataccen sakamako (yana amfani da daidaitaccen taswirar Claude Code idan akwai, in ba haka ba PascalCase), sannan yana mayar da sunayen a martani ta `_toolNameMap`, domin kada a ƙi harnesses masu kayan aikin snake_case a matsayin abokan ciniki na ɓangare na uku da aka gane ta sawun yatsa. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.142.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Lokacin da OpenAI ta sabunta Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.131.0` | Sauya sigar abokin cinikin Codex ba tare da dogaro da cikakken rubutun UA ba |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.258` | Sauya sigar Claude Code da ake sanarwa ba tare da dogaro da `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` ba. Anthropic tana taƙaita wasu samfura bisa wannan ƙimar (#12417). |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.81-6` | Sauya sigar Copilot CLI da ake sanarwa ba tare da dogaro da `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` ba |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Lokacin da GitHub Copilot Chat ya sabunta |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Lokacin da Antigravity IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Lokacin da Kiro IDE ya sabunta |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Sauya `clientId` (ID na jama'a) na lambar na'urar zamantakewa ta Kiro |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Zaɓi na shiga: cikakken tabbatar da CRC na saƙon kowane frame a rafin event na Kiro (don gano matsalar gurɓatattun rafuka) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Lokacin da Qoder CLI ya sabunta |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Lokacin da Cursor ya sabunta |

> [!TIP]
> Za ka iya ƙara sauya saitunan User-Agent ga **kowane** mai bayarwa ta amfani da tsarin `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor yana ƙirƙirar sunan env var ta atomatik.
