## 13. Daidaituwar Sawun Yatsa na CLI

Idan aka kunna, OmniRoute yana sake tsara kanun HTTP da filayen jikin JSON domin su yi daidai da takamaiman sa hannun kayan aikin CLI na hukuma. Wannan yana rage haɗarin yi wa asusunku alama, tare da kiyaye IP na proxy ɗinku.

**Tushe:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Bisa ga Kowane Mai Bayarwa

| Sauyi                    | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                           |
| ------------------------ | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`    | Yana kwaikwayon sa hannun buƙatar Cline          |

### Na Gabaɗaya

| Sauyi            | Kunnawa | Tasiri                                                               |
| ---------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`    | Kunna daidaituwar sawun yatsa ga **duk** masu bayarwa a lokaci guda. |

### Sauya bayanan asalin Kimi Coding CLI

| Sauyi                   | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                          | Bayani                                                                                |
| ----------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`                | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya sigar Kimi CLI da ake aikawa yayin kiran OAuth/API.                             |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(tsohon da aka ɗauka)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Sauya ID na na'urar Kimi da aka ɗauka, wanda ake amfani da shi a kanun abokin ciniki. |

> [!NOTE]
> Wannan fasalin yana aiki tare da sauye-sauyen User-Agent (§12). Tsarin sawun yatsa yana sarrafa jeren kanun da jeren filayen jiki, yayin da sauye-sauyen User-Agent ke sarrafa takamaiman zaren UA. Ana iya kunna kowannensu daban.

---

## 14. Masu Bayar da Maɓallin API

Maɓallan API na masu bayarwa waɗanda ke amfani da tantancewa kai tsaye. **Saitin da aka fi so:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Saitawa ta hanyar sauye-sauyen muhalli wata hanya ce ta daban ga turawa ta Docker ko tsarin da ba shi da mahaɗar gani.

Tsarin da ake gane shi: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Sauyi              | Mai Bayarwa                                         |
| ------------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                            |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                          |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + madadin Reader)           |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (wani suna na `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)            |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + madadin taɗi |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (wani suna na `GEMINI_API_KEY`)              |

> [!NOTE]
> An cire tsayayyun shigarwar `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` na Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, da Qianfan a v3.8.0, saboda tsarin aiki ba ya ƙara karanta su — waɗannan masu bayarwa suna dogara ne kawai da Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / rumbun bayanai mai ɓoyewa. Duba sashen _Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Aiki_ a ƙasan wannan takarda domin hanyar ƙaura.

> [!TIP]
> Maɓallan da aka saita ta Dashboard ana adana su a SQLite cikin ɓoyayyen tsari, kuma su ne ke da fifiko a kan sauye-sauyen muhalli.
>
> **Jina:** `jina-ai/…` embeddings, sake jera matsayi, rarrabewa, rabewa, da `jina-search` ba sa cajar maɓallin muhallin cluster idan akwai haɗin dashboard na `jina-ai` (ko `jina-reader` na bai ɗaya) — `getProviderCredentials` yana fara cike bayanai ne da abin da ya fara samu. Ana amfani da `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta amfani da maɓallin muhalli na madadin da `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Katin Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ba ya taɓa hidimar `/v1/embeddings` ko `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (wani suna `google/gemini-embedding-2`) yana fara amfani da haɗin dashboard na `gemini`. Ana amfani da `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ne kawai idan babu maɓallin dashboard mai amfani. Rajistan kira yana danganta amfani da maɓallin muhalli na madadin da `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Zirga-zirgar multimodal ta asali tana amfani da `x-goog-api-key` zuwa `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — abubuwan `input` guda N na OpenAI suna zama vectors guda N.

---

## 15. Saitunan Ƙarewar Lokaci

Duk ƙimomin suna cikin **milliseƙonni**. An tattara warware saitunan a `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Tsarin Gadon Ƙarewar Lokaci

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (saitin maye gurbin gama-gari)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (kiran masu samarwa na waje, tsoho: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 15000)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (mai zaman kansa, tsoho: 1260000; 0 yana kashewa)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (yana iyakance tsawaitawar shiri mai daidaituwa, tsoho: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (yana gado daga REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, tsoho: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (an samo shi, tsoho: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (tsoho: 0 = a kashe)
```

| Maɓalli                                         | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Gajeriyar hanya ta gaba ɗaya — tana maye gurbin tsoffin ƙimomin `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Jimillar wa'adin ƙarewar buƙatar HTTP don kiran masu samar da sabis na sama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin tsawon shiru tsakanin ɗanyen bytes na sama kafin a katse. Samfuran tunani mai zurfi ba safai suke dakatawa >90s ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Matsakaicin jimillar tsawon rayuwar rafi na SSE mai aiki; ba ya sake farawa idan bytes na sama sun iso kuma baya dogara da `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. An samo shi daga mafi girman `timeoutMs` na kowane samfuri a rajista (1200000, Codex) tare da ƙarin tazara ta 60000, don haka ba za a taɓa kashe samfurin da aka bari ya yi amfani da cikakken kasafin lokacinsa a tsakiyar amsa ba. Saita zuwa `0` don kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(a kashe)_                                                       | Ko OmniRoute na iya fitar da layukan sharhin SSE na `:` (misali bugun zuciyar `: keepalive` da ƙarin bayanan `x-omniroute-*` a ƙarshen saƙo). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa (#10524) saboda tsauraran abokan ciniki masu dacewa da OpenAI suna yin JSON.parse ga kowane layin SSE kuma su rushe kan sharhin `:`; bugun zuciya na `data:` bai shafa ba. Saita `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` don sake shiga. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Lokacin jira don karɓar aukuwar SSE ta farko wadda ba ping ba. Yana gado daga `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan an saita shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga mai daidaitawa na jiran aukuwar farko don manyan buƙatun yawo, masu yawan amfani da kayan aiki, ko masu zurfin tunani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Maɓallin kashe gaggawa na dabarar `/goal`. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe ganowa gaba ɗaya — ba a taɓa ƙara wa'adin shirye-shirye da farfaɗo da rafi saboda jikin buƙata/headers, wanda ke rage yiwuwar abokin ciniki ya tsawaita wa'adin ƙarewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Matsakaicin taga na jiran aukuwar farko don tafiyar wakilin `/goal` da aka gano ko buƙatun da aka tilasta da `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kunna farfaɗo da rafi da wuri ta atomatik don tafiyar wakilin `/goal` da aka gano. Saita `false`/`0`/`off` don kashe zaɓin shiga na musamman ga goal. Wannan zai iya ƘARA farfaɗowa ne kawai a kan tsohon saitin mai gudanarwa — ba zai taɓa soke fitowar da aka bayyana kai tsaye ta saitunan `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Cire aukuwar SSE na `codex.*` marasa daidaito (misali `codex.rate_limits`) waɗanda ke karya `responses.stream()` na OpenAI SDK da 502. A KUNNE yake ta tsohuwa (#11014). Saita `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` don tura su gaba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar sufuri ta Codex app-server da ake shiga bisa zaɓi. Wurin haɗin WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) na sidecar na `codex app-server` na gida. Idan an saita shi tare da token, ana tura buƙatun Codex ta JSON-RPC zuwa sidecar maimakon HTTP Responses API. Haka kuma ana iya saita shi ga kowace haɗi ta `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` ne ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Token na capability/bearer da aka saka kai tsaye wanda ake miƙawa app-server. Maye gurbi na kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Hanyar fayil mai ɗauke da token na capability na app-server (daga `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). Ana amfani da shi idan ba a saita `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` ba. Maye gurbi na kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Kundin aiki da zagayen app-server ke gudana a ciki. Maye gurbi na kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar amincewa da ake miƙawa zagayen app-server (misali `never`, `on-request`). Maye gurbi na kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Manufar sandbox da ake miƙawa zagayen app-server (misali `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Idan ba a saita ba executor zai yi amfani da `workspace-write` a matsayin tsoho (an ƙarfafa tsaro; a baya `danger-full-access`). Maye gurbi na kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Amincewa ta atomatik da buƙatun amincewa na app-server kansa (aiwatar da umarni/fayil/izini a kan host). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — ana ƙin buƙatun ta atomatik; kiran kayan aikin harness bai shafa ba (suna bi ta hanyar wucewa ta daban ta `item/tool/call`). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`yes`. Maye gurbi na kowace haɗi: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin jira don karɓar headers na amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Matsakaicin lokacin jiran fara amsa (ms) ga kowane yunƙurin kai-tsaye ba tare da proxy ba. Idan lokaci ya ƙare, ana sake gwadawa sau ɗaya a kan sabon socket; saita `0` don kashe iyakar kuma a riƙe halayyar da ta gabata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (minti 10)                                               | Matsakaicin iyaka (ms) ga yunƙurin RETRY na sabon socket bayan lokacin jiran fara amsa na yunƙurin pooled da ke sama ya ƙare (#13703). Yana aiki ne kawai idan mai kira ya riga ya haɗa nasa siginar wa'adin ƙarewa (jerin fifikon connection/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` da aka tantance); wannan siginar ce ainihin iyaka kuma ita ce ke fara aiki a hanyar da aka nufa, don haka wannan kariya ce mai yalwa maimakon tsayayyen iyaka — idan babu ita, sake-gwajin yana sake amfani da gajeriyar taga ɗaya ta `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` kamar yunƙurin pooled, abin da ke haifar da 504 ga lafiyayyun reasoning models masu jinkirin TTFB. Ba a taɓa yarda ya sauka ƙasa da tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar da ke sama ba; idan mai kira bai samar da kowace siginar wa'adin ƙarewa ba, sake-gwajin zai bar tsayayyen mafi ƙarancin iyakar yadda yake. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin karɓar cikakken jikin amsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar kafa haɗin TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewar zaman banza na keep-alive socket.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Lokacin ƙarewar TLS fingerprint proxy (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Yana iyakance lokacin zuwa byte na farko musamman a jikin jigilar TLS-fingerprint ta wreq-js; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` shi kaɗai ba zai iya gano jikin da ya tsaya cik ba, domin yana warwarewa da zarar headers sun iso (#12656). Idan lokaci ya ƙare, ana soke wreq reader sannan a koma ga direct/proxy dispatcher; `0` yana kashe watchdog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Na OpenCode executor kawai, kuma kawai yayin da feature flag na `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` yake kunne (a kashe yake ta tsohuwa): yana iyakance jiran byte na farko na jikin streamed Responses reply bayan headers ɗinsa (#13484). Responses stream yana buɗewa da `response.created`, don haka shiru bayan wannan taga alamar tsayawa cik ne: ana sanya account ɗin cikin lokacin hucewa sannan a juya request zuwa account na gaba sau ɗaya; tsayawa cik na biyu yana sa a gaza nan take. `0` yana kashe kariyar ko da flag ɗin yana kunne.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar matakin proxy ga requests na bridge na `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Nuna Firecrawl web-fetch executor zuwa instance da aka self-host (API key ba dole ba ne idan ba a cloud ba).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewa ga kowane request na Firecrawl web-fetch executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Jimillar lokacin ƙarewar server request ga bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Lokacin aika response headers ta bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewar zaman banza na keep-alive na bridge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Lokacin ƙarewar raw socket (0 = a kashe).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin sassauci bayan SIGTERM/SIGINT kafin tilasta fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Fallback da `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` ke amfani da shi idan ba a saita `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Lokacin ƙarewa (ms) ga presets na `validationRead` da `modelsProbe` a cikin `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Ƙara shi ga endpoints masu jinkiri (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) don hana yawan sauyawa tsakanin active/error a dashboard. Yana komawa 8000ms ga ƙimomin da ba su inganta ba (<1000) ko waɗanda ba lambobi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar fetch na musamman ga relay a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Dole ne relay da ya maƙale ya gaza kafin lokacin ƙarewar client/agent (~30s), domin masu kira su ga gazawa ta musamman ga relay maimakon lokacin ƙarewar upstream na gama-gari. An iyakance shi zuwa `29000` domin koyaushe ya fara aiki.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Lokacin jira na retry da aka raba don hanyoyin sake-gwadawa-sau-ɗaya na direct/relay/proxy a cikin `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwame na cikakken wa'adin JS da ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin ƙarewa na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwame na cikakken wa'adin JS da ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin ƙarewa na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (a kashe)                                                     | Yana liƙa "Kana da ginanniyar binciken yanar gizo. Amsa tambayoyi kai tsaye ta amfani da sakamakon bincike." a saƙon tsarin mai kira (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — Perplexity yana bincike ko ta yaya, kuma jimlar tana bayyana a cikin amsoshi a matsayin sharhin-meta ga abokan hulɗar coding. Saita `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` don maidowa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwame na cikakken wa'adin JS da ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin ƙarewa na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar wreq-js na asali (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` yana ɗaga shi zuwa `180000` ga kowace buƙata don dogayen samarwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ƙarin lokacin rangwame na cikakken wa'adin JS da ake ƙarawa a kan lokacin ƙarewa na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Rumbun burauzar Playwright na bai ɗaya don tattaunawar kuki-na-yanar-gizo mai amfani da burauza (`browserPool.ts`); saita `off` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Hanya zuwa fayil ɗin binary na `obscura` da rumbun burauza da mai aiwatar da Cloudflare Playground ke amfani da shi a matsayin injin farko (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); ana gano shi ta atomatik daga PATH na tsarin idan ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                 | Nuna zuwa Obscura da ya riga yake aiki (`http://host:port`) maimakon ƙaddamar da ɗaya; modulin ba shi ne ke mallakar wannan tsari ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Tashar da aka ayyana kai tsaye don `obscura serve` da aka ƙaddamar; ana zaɓar tashar da ba a amfani da ita ta atomatik idan ba a saita ba (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Zaɓar shigar da buƙatar tattaunawar kuki-na-yanar-gizo cikin hanyar da ke amfani da burauza (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` don kunnawa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL na tushe ga mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (kimi.ai Connect-RPC na ƙasa da ƙasa) (`kimi-web.ts`); sauya shi kawai don wuraren ƙarshen madubi/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Cikakken wurin ƙarshen tattaunawa ga mai aiwatar da Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(ana ganowa ta atomatik)_                                        | Hanya zuwa fayil ɗin aiwatarwa na Chrome/Edge na tsarin don shiga ta burauza mai hulɗa ta Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); tana rinjayar ganowa ta atomatik bisa OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ fitarwa mai zaman kanta_                                 | Sauyin lokacin gini don kundin adireshin fitarwa mai zaman kansa da matakin haɗa-wuri bayan gini ke amfani da shi (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); kayan aikin gini ne, ba na lokacin aiki ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Ƙoƙarin maƙasudin combo suna gadon lokacin ƙarewar buƙatar upstream da aka warware (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ko
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` idan shi ne ke samar da tsohuwar ƙimar fetch). Saita `targetTimeoutMs` a cikin combo,
tsoffin ƙimomin combo, ko sauyin mai samarwa kawai don sa komawar combo ta yi sauri; ƙimomin da suka haura
lokacin ƙarewar upstream na yanzu ana iyakance su zuwa lokacin ƙarewar upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` kasafin lokacin agogo ne na dukkan combo gaba ɗaya, daban daga na maƙasudan failover.
Bar shi ba a saita ba ko saita shi zuwa `0` don ci gaba da maimaitawa ba tare da iyaka ba (har yanzu
matakin dakatar da makalewa na minti 10 na `COMBO_LOOP_SAFETY_TIMEOUT_MS` yana aiki). Ƙima mai kyau tana maye gurbin wannan
kariyar ga combo. Ka sa `comboTimeoutMs` ya fi `targetTimeoutMs` tsawo domin failover
ya ci gaba da samun lokaci bayan jinkirin maƙasudi na farko.

### Iyakokin Mai Katse Da'ira

Daidaita mai katse da'ira a matakin mai samarwa. Tsoffin ƙimomi suna nuna ƙimomin da aka faɗaɗa da ake amfani da su tun daga v3.6 don haɗin kai 500+.

| Maɓalli                                                       | Tsoho     | Fayil ɗin Tushe                | Bayani                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samar da OAuth kafin mai katsewar ya kunna.                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Lokacin sake saiti (ms) na mai katsewar mai samar da OAuth.                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samarwa masu amfani da API-key.                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Lokacin sake saiti (ms) na mai katsewar mai samar da API-key.                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Iyakar gazawa a jere ga masu samarwa na gida (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Lokacin sake saiti (ms) na mai katsewar mai samarwa na gida.                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa a cikin lokacin kafin dukkan mai samar da OAuth ya shiga lokacin hucewa.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: taga mai motsi ta ƙirga gazawa (ms) ga masu samar da OAuth.                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: lokacin hucewa (ms) da zarar an kai iyakar mai samar da OAuth.                                                             |

| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` | Mai katsewa a matakin mai samarwa: yawan gazawa a cikin lokacin kafin dukkan mai samar da API-key ya shiga lokacin hucewa.                                    |

| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`   | Zurfin ja-da-baya inda ake ɗaukar mai samar da fil ɗin ma'ajiyar mahallin a matsayin marar lafiya na dindindin, sannan a sauke fil ɗin don sauya zuwa madadi. |

## 16. Yin rajista

Tsarin yin rajista yana rubutawa zuwa stdout da kuma fayilolin rajista masu juyawa. Ana karanta dukkan saituna daga `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Sauyi                                     | Tsoho                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Mafi ƙarancin matakin rajista: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Tsarin fitarwa: `text` (wanda ɗan Adam zai iya karantawa) ko `json` (mai tsari).                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Rubuta rajista zuwa fayil tare da stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Hanyar fayil ɗin rajista (dangane da tushen aikin ko `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Matsakaicin girman fayil kafin juyawa. Yana karɓar: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ko bytes kai tsaye.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Kwanakin da za a adana fayilolin rajistar manhaja da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Matsakaicin adadin kwafin ajiyar fayilolin rajista da aka juya.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Kwanakin da za a adana shigarwar rajistar buƙata/kira a cikin ma'ajiyar bayanai.                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Matsakaicin shigarwar rajistar kira a cikin ma'ajiyar wucin gadi ta ƙwaƙwalwa.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin layuka a cikin teburin SQLite na `call_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_          | Tilasta kunna ko kashe cikakken rajistar buƙata, tare da yin watsi da saitin dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (awa 1)          | Matsakaicin shekarun shigarwar rajistar buƙata mai aiki da ta zama marar alaƙa kafin tsaftacewar ƙwaƙwalwa.                                                                                                                                               |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Adana gutsattsarin rafi a cikin kayan pipeline lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Sai an zaɓa (`true`) — a kashe yake ta tsohuwa don adana sararin faifai.                                                                                      |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Matsakaicin girman kayan rajistar kiran pipeline a KB lokacin da `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Matsakaicin layuka a cikin teburin SQLite na `proxy_logs` kafin rage su.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Haɗa IP na abokin ciniki/fitarwa da farkon sunayen asusu a cikin rajistar console na `[ProxyEgress]`. Bayanan rajistar proxy na dashboard/ma'ajiyar bayanai suna riƙe cikakkun bayanai.                                                                   |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (minti 1)          | Sau nawa `src/lib/logRotation.ts` ke sake duba girman fayil ɗin rajista mai aiki.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Matsakaicin tsawon kirtani da ake riƙewa a cikin kayan rajistar taɗi (tsoho 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Adadin abubuwan array da ake riƙewa daga ƙarshensa yayin gajarta bayanan rajistar taɗi.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Matsakaicin zurfin ɗaurin ciki kafin a gajarta bayanan rajistar taɗi.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Matsakaicin maɓallan object da ake riƙewa a cikin bayanan rajistar taɗi (0 = babu iyaka).                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Girman gaba ɗaya na jikin buƙata/amsa (KB) wanda idan ya wuce za a maye gurbinsa da taƙaitaccen bayani kawai maimakon cikakken kwafinsa. Ƙara wannan idan dogayen tattaunawa na wakilai suna nuna alamar wucin gadi maimakon ainihin saƙonni a dashboard. |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Idan true ne, `serializeArtifactForStorage` yana tsallake gajartawa bisa girma. Don gyaran kurakurai kawai.                                                                                                                                               |
