## 17. Inganta Amfani da Ƙwaƙwalwa

| Maɓalli                       | Tsoho                                                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _ta atomatik_ (na'ura kai tsaye); **`1024` a cikin hoton Docker** | **Iyakar heap ta V8 da ake ba da shawara** (MB) don Docker/na'ura mai zaman kanta. Idan ba a saita ba, ana daidaita ta ta atomatik (~35% na RAM na tsarin, tare da iyakancewa zuwa `[512, 4096]`); `512` shi ne mafi ƙarancin ƙima kawai idan ba za a iya karanta jimillar ƙwaƙwalwar ba. A kan `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), ana ƙara ƙimar da aka **saita kai tsaye** a matsayin `--max-old-space-size`, kuma ita ce ke **rinjayar** tutar heap ta NODE_OPTIONS da ke saɓani da ita (V8 na amfani da tuta ta ƙarshe). Har yanzu `omniroute serve` yana fifita tutar heap ta NODE_OPTIONS da ta riga ta kasance (#5238). Kada a saita su biyun zuwa lambobi daban-daban — tsarin yana rubuta gargaɗi da ke ambaton ƙimomin biyu da kuma wadda ta yi rinjaye. **Hoton Docker na hukuma koyaushe yana saita `1024`, don haka ba a taɓa gudanar da daidaitawar a cikinsa.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` yana buƙatar `8192`–`12288` tare da ƙarin sararin cgroup — duba [Jagorar Docker — RAM na lokacin aiki](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                              | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar system prompt da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                  | Matsakaicin jimillar girman cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (minti 5)                                                | TTL na shigarwar cache na prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                             | Matsakaicin adadin martani masu temperature=0 da aka adana a cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                  | Matsakaicin jimillar girman semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (minti 30)                                              | TTL na shigarwar semantic cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                               | Tushen local-corpus da ke riƙe da index mai aiki a ƙwaƙwalwa lokaci guda (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: idan an kai iyakar, ana cire index na tushen da aka fi daɗewa ba a yi amfani da shi ba, sannan a sake gina shi lokacin tambayarsa ta gaba. Ana iyakance shi zuwa mafi ƙarancin `1`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba tana komawa zuwa ƙimar tsoho.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                              | Matsakaicin adadin abubuwan stream na baya-bayan nan a cikin buffer na kallon kai-tsaye na Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | `128000`                                                          | Matsakaicin tsawon context na madadin gama-gari ga model waɗanda ba su da takamaiman config.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                             | Ƙarin sararin token da aka tanada yayin bin diddigin iyakokin amfani.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Matsawa

| Maɓalli                               | Tsoho         | Bayani                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | ba a saita ba | Amince da `.rtk/filters.json` na project ba tare da hash na `.rtk/trust.json` ba. Yi amfani da wannan kawai a cikin ci gaban gida mai sarrafawa. |

### Injin Ƙwaƙwalwa (shiri na 21)

### Kuɗin event-loop na ƙwaƙwalwa, skills, da sabunta token (#10349)

OmniRoute **tsarin Node guda ɗaya ne**. Cirowa/dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa, shigar da skills, da sabunta token na provider suna gudana a kan **event loop ɗin nan ɗaya** da `GET /healthz` da dashboard. Ba worker thread ba ne.

| Aiki                     | Lamba                              | Tsoho                                               | Ikon mai gudanarwa                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------ | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Ciro / dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa | `src/lib/memory/`                  | **memoryEnabled** na Dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory**. Babu wani keɓantaccen maɓallin kashewa na env bayan kashe fasalin a cikin saituna.                                      |
| Shigar da ƙwarewa        | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | **skillsEnabled** na Dashboard (a kunne ta tsohuwa) | Kashe **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Saitunan sandbox da ke ƙasa suna iyakance aiwatarwa ne kawai bayan an riga an kunna shigarwar. |
| Sabunta token            | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | A kunne ga masu samar da OAuth/web da aka haɗa      | Cire haɗin mai samarwa ko bar tokens su ci gaba da zama ingantattu; a halin yanzu babu env na `TOKEN_REFRESH=0`.                                     |

Idan `/healthz` yana tafiyar hawainiya a kan na'ura marar wani aiki, fara da kashe memory + skills, sannan a duba nauyin catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Waɗannan fasaloli suna ba da dama a wuraren `await`, amma har yanzu suna gasa kan thread guda ɗaya.

Saitunan embedding layer, vector store da reranking na tsarin ƙwaƙwalwa mai ɗorewa (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Variable                             | Tsoho                       | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (minti 5)          | TTL na cache ɗin embedding da ke cikin ƙwaƙwalwar na'ura (ga kowane sa hannun source/model/dim).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Matsakaicin adadin shigarwar LRU da ake riƙewa a cikin cache ɗin embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id na pipeline na MiniLM na cikin gida na `@huggingface/transformers` da ake zaɓar kunnawa (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id na static potion/Model2Vec lookup-table embedder. Ana sauke shi a hankali zuwa cikin cache dir idan an buƙata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Kundin adireshin da ake amfani da shi don cache na fayilolin static potion model. Yana komawa ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR` ta tsohuwa idan ba a saita shi ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Sauya tushen URL na Hugging Face Hub da `staticPotion.ts` ke amfani da shi (misali, mirror endpoint don saitunan da aka ware daga intanet).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Tsohon top-K da binciken vector na brute-force na `sqlite-vec` ke amfani da shi a cikin `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Ƙimar dindindin `k` ta Reciprocal Rank Fusion don haɗaɗɗen dawo da bayanai na FTS5 + vector (tsarin sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Wurin gwaji/bincike a cikin `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): idan `true`, yana tilasta vector store ya zama `null` (yana kwaikwayon muhallin cloud/WASM marar `sqlite-vec`), yana rage dawo da ƙwaƙwalwa zuwa binciken keyword na FTS5. A bar shi ba tare da saiti ba a production.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_           | API key na Notion backend (`genericBackend.ts` known backend preset ne ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Tushen URL na Notion API (ana iya sauya shi don madadin Notion da ake host da kai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_           | API key na Obsidian Vault backend (`genericBackend.ts` known backend preset ne ke amfani da shi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Tushen URL na Obsidian Vault API (ana iya sauya shi don vault na nesa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Babban maɓallin TV6 typed memory decay. **Zaɓin kunnawa ne (a kashe ta tsohuwa)** — sweep ɗin yana **goge** ƙwaƙwalwar da ta lalace. Idan yana kashe, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bayanan sa ido ne kawai kuma ba a taɓa goge komai ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (kwanaki) wanda bayan sa ƙwaƙwalwar `episodic` da ba a yi amfani da ita ba za ta lalace. `0` yana sa episodic ma ta zama kariyayya. Nau'ikan masu ɗorewa (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) koyaushe suna da kariya. Ana sake kafa agogon lalacewa bisa `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Ƙwaƙwalwar da aka shigar sau `>=` wannan adadi tana samun kariya daga lalacewa ba tare da la'akari da nau'inta ba. `0` yana kashe kariyar samun dama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (a kashe)               | Tazara (daƙiƙa) don sweep na lalacewa na lokaci-lokaci na zaɓi a cikin `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ba a saita ba = babu sweep na lokaci-lokaci. Ana buƙatar zaɓin kunnawa sau biyu: yana kuma buƙatar `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(ba a saita ba)_           | Provider ids da aka raba da wakafi (ba tare da la'akari da manyan ko ƙananan haruffa ba) waɗanda ke karɓar saƙon `system` **a index 0 kawai** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Ga waɗannan, haɗa ƙwaƙwalwa a tsakiyar array cikin hanyar da ke kiyaye cache ba ta da aminci a tattaunawa masu zagaye da yawa, don haka ana haɗa/ƙara ƙwaƙwalwar a gaba a matsayin saƙon system na farko. Ta tsohuwa `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` kawai; faɗaɗa shi don endpoints masu dacewa da OpenAI da ake host da kai (misali Qwen3.5+/3.6), waɗanda chat template ɗinsu ke tilasta irin wannan ƙa'idar saƙon system guda ɗaya a gaba. |

### Misalin Docker Mai Ƙarancin RAM

`128` na dashboard kaɗai ne. Coding agents a kan wannan heap suna samun `FATAL ERROR` yayin dogon `/v1/responses`. Kada a yi amfani da wannan misalin a matsayin gateway na Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## Daidaita ELO na Arena

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho         | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                      | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Daidaita ELO na jadawalin jagororin Arena AI lokaci-lokaci, wanda za a iya saita shi daga Dashboard Feature Flags ko a kashe shi da `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Tsarin prefix da ake amfani da shi don id na samfura a cikin `GET /v1/models`. `dual` yana nuna duka gajeren prefix na alias da cikakken prefix na mai samarwa ga kowane samfuri (dacewa da sigogin baya — kusan yana ninka kundin sau biyu); `alias` yana fitar da id guda ɗaya ga kowane samfuri; `canonical` yana fitar da cikakken prefix na provider-id kawai (masu samarwa waɗanda alias ɗinsu ya riga ya zama canonical id suna riƙe shigarwarsu guda ɗaya). Abokan hulɗa za su iya maye gurbin wannan a kowace buƙata da `?prefix=alias`. Duba [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Tazarar daidaitawa a cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

## Mai Samar da PromptQL Playground (Ba na Hukuma ba/Na Gwaji)

Gadar zaman GraphQL da aka yi reverse-engineering don prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Dukansu na zaɓi ne — tsoffin ƙimomi suna nuni zuwa endpoints na playground na jama'a; a sauya su kawai don tura PromptQL da aka karɓi bakunci da kai ko wani madadin tura shi.

| Sauyi                        | Tsoho                                                              | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                         | Bayani                                                                            |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint na GraphQL da ake amfani da shi don ayyukan taɗi/zama.                   |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | Endpoint na GraphQL da ake amfani da shi don neman ma'aunin kuɗin kiredit/amfani. |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint da ake amfani da shi don sabunta token gwargwadon iko.                   |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Matsakaicin lokaci (ms) na duba `thread_events` kafin lokacin jira ya ƙare.       |

## Mai Samar da Yanar Gizo na HyperAgent (Ba na Hukuma ba/Na Gwaji)

Gadar zaman da aka gano tsarin aikinta ta hanyar nazarin baya don hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — tsohon saitin yana nuna zuwa wurin ƙarshen lissafin kuɗi/amfani na jama'a; a sauya shi kawai don turawar HyperAgent da aka ɗauki nauyinta da kai ko wata turawa ta daban.

| `HYPERAGENT_USAGE_URL` | `https://hyperagent.com/api/settings/billing/usage` | `open-sse/services/usage/hyperagent.ts` | Wurin ƙarshen da ake amfani da shi don ɗauko tubalan kiredit na lissafin kuɗi/amfani. |

## Ƙayyadaddun Amfani na Kilo Code

Binciken ragowar kuɗin USD na mutum da amfanin Kilo Pass don mai samar da Kilo Code. Na zaɓi ne — tsohon saitin yana nuna zuwa Kilo API na jama'a; a sauya shi kawai don relay/kayan gwaji. Tantancewa tana amfani da alamar samun dama ta OAuth da ke akwai a haɗin.

| Sauyi          | Tsohon Saiti          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                       | Bayani                                                                                             |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | URL na tushe da ake amfani da shi don ɗauko ragowar kuɗin Kilo Code na mutum da amfanin Kilo Pass. |

## Mai Samar da Yanar Gizo na Adobe Firefly (Ba na Hukuma ba/Na Gwaji)

Sabunta zaman da burauza ke gudanarwa don mai samar da yanar gizo na Adobe Firefly
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Na zaɓi ne — an daidaita duk tsoffin saituna don shigarwar
kwamfutar tebur ta yau da kullum.

> **An cire a #9255.** Tsohon tsarin gudanar da Chrome mai haɗin CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) an
> maye gurbinsa da sabis na shiga ta burauza na Playwright, kuma saitunansa ba su wanzu. Sauye-sauyen
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tare da
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS da ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ba a karanta su a ko'ina cikin
> kundin lambar — saita su ba shi da wani tasiri.

| Sauyi                                | Tsohon Saiti | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                 | Bayani                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------ | ----------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Saita zuwa `1` don Chrome marar fuskar mai amfani na ainihi (an san ba ya aiki don samarwa; don gyaran kurakurai kawai).                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Zaɓin kunna/kashe ɗumama burauza tun da wuri. `0` yana kashe ɗumama tun da wuri (dawo da aiki daga 408 a tsakiyar rukuni har yanzu yana aiki). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`          | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Saita zuwa `0` don hana adana zaman Adobe Firefly a kan faifai.                                                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(unset)_    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Mafi ƙarancin tazara (ms) da ake tilastawa tsakanin turawa jere, wanda ke maye gurbin tsohon saitin da aka gina a ciki.                        |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(unset)_    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ƙarin tazara (ms) da ake ƙarawa bayan nasarar rukuni, wanda ke maye gurbin tsohon saitin da aka gina a ciki.                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(unset)_    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Jinkirin tushe (ms) kafin aika buƙatar samarwa, wanda ke maye gurbin tsohon saitin da aka gina a ciki.                                         |

## 19. Daidaita Samfuri (Dev)

| Sauyi                               | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tilasta kunna ko kashe daidaita farashin models.dev. Idan ba a saita ba = a bi Settings > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **yana rinjayar DB** kuma yana tsallake daidaitawa ta lokaci-lokaci da binciken SQL/JSON na `getModelsDevPricing()` (don farfaɗowa lokacin da dashboard ya maƙale a event loop ɗaya). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` yana tilasta kunna daidaitawa. Ajiye/share farashi har yanzu yana kiran `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, wanda ba ya yin komai a ƙarƙashin takunkumin mintuna 60 ko `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (awa 24)  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Tazarar daidaita kundin samfura yayin haɓakawa, cikin daƙiƙu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (awa 24)  | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Tazara (daƙiƙu) ga mai daidaita context-window mai gyara kansa (5004): yana kafa windows da mai samarwa ya ayyana daga gano `/models` a matsayin overrides na `auto:discovery` idan sun bambanta da kundin. Saita zuwa `0` don kashewa. Yana sake amfani da bayanan da aka riga aka daidaita (babu sabon fetch); ba ya taɓa maye gurbin overrides na `manual`.                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Rumbun bayanansa yana ɗauke da bayanan shaidar OAuth/API na mai samarwa, kuma hoton da aka ƙayyade ba shi da hanyar sauya `api-keys` na ɓangaren bayanai ta env (sai dai `config.yaml` da aka ɗora), don haka `0.0.0.0` yana fallasa sabis mai ɗauke da bayanan shaida ga dukkan LAN. |

| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                            | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Mai masaukin ɗaurewa/haɗawa na ginannen sabis na Dario (loopback kawai a matsayin tsoho).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                 | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Tashar ginannen sabis na Dario.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                            | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Mai masaukin ɗaurewa/haɗawa na ginannen sabis na Dario (loopback kawai a matsayin tsoho).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                 | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Tashar ginannen sabis na Dario.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(babu komai)_                         | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Ƙarin sunayen masu masaukin da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ake ɗauka a matsayin na "gida" (sunayen sabis na Docker, da sauransu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` ana amfani da shi ne kawai don relays na wasu kamfanoni waɗanda ke karɓar abokan hulɗar Claude Code
kaɗai. OmniRoute yana sake tsara buƙatun domin waɗannan relays su karɓe su. Idan kana son amfani da
Claude Code CLI kawai, ko kuma ba ka da tabbacin abin da waɗannan relays suke nufi, bar wannan a kashe sannan ka ƙara mai bayar da sabis na yau da kullum
wanda ya dace da Anthropic a maimakonsa.

Yana riƙe tagar buɗe SSE har zuwa `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms), ta yadda yanke na _kafin-commit_ — wanda ke faruwa kafin kowane byte ya isa ga abokin ciniki — za a sake buɗe shi kuma a sake gwadawa ba tare da an gani ba. **Lokacin kunnawa:** idan upstreams ba su da tabbas/suna yawan yankewa da byte 0 a farkon rafi; a bar shi a KASHE idan ba za ku iya ɗaukar ƙarin jinkirin da zai kai 750 ms kafin token na farko a kowane rafi ba. Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`. Yana samar da ƙimar farko ga saitin Resilience da ake adanawa; saitin Dashboard ne ke rinjaye da zarar an saita shi. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (ƙimar farko) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (dabaru) | **Mene ne:** ci gaba daga tsakiyar rafi (Mataki 4.4) — bayan yankewar _bayan-commit_ (bytes sun riga sun isa ga abokin ciniki), a sake aika buƙata tare da ɓangaren rubutun a matsayin assistant prefill sannan a haɗa ƙarshen da ya ɓace. Don rafukan rubutu-tsantsa masu dacewa da OpenAI kawai; ba ya taɓa aiki yayin da ake gudanar da tool call. **Lokacin kunnawa:** don dogayen samar da amsa da ake yankewa a tsakiyar amsa, kuma kun yarda ɓangaren ƙarshen da aka dawo da shi ya zo lokaci guda maimakon token-bi-token. Ba ya dogara da `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (yana da tsarin haɗari daban). Yana karɓar `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                                    |

Yana gano rafukan da ke ci gaba da aika guntu-guntu amma suka kasance ƙasa da ƙimar fitarwar assistant da aka saita; heartbeats, al’amuran amfani, empty deltas, da matakan kayan aiki/tunani ba sa bayyana a matsayin ci gaba na ƙarya. Ya bambanta da iyakokin lokacin zaman banza da na ƙarshe marar sassauci.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                                   | Matsakaicin jitter na bazuwar lokaci (ms) da ake ƙarawa a kan `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` tsakanin binciken lafiyar token na masu samarwa, don hana taruwa lokaci guda (Batu #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                         | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                        | Adadin sake-gwada ta atomatik kan martanin lokacin jira da ya shafi model kafin a mayar da kuskure ga client.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                        | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                        | Matsakaicin tazarar backoff (daƙiƙa) tsakanin sake-gwada bayan lokacin jira. Wannan ƙimar ce ke iyakance shi ba tare da la’akari da `Retry-After` na upstream ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`     | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                                    | URL na proxy mai adana token na Headroom. Zagayen rayuwar dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) yana ƙaddamar da CLI na `headroom-ai` na gida a kan loopback ta tsohuwa; a sauya wannan kawai don nuna wani proxy na Docker sidecar na waje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

### Konstantocin daidaita dawo da stream

Ana daidaita halayyar jinkirta dawo da aiki ta hanyar konstantoci da aka hardcode a cikin
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), waɗanda aka nuna a nan don tunani —
sauya su yana buƙatar gyaran code, ba env var ba:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — tsawon lokacin da ake riƙe taga ta farkon SSE
  domin a sake gwadawa idan an yanke ta da wuri kafin a aika ko da byte ɗaya zuwa ga client.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — iyaka tabbatacciya ga tagar da aka riƙe; a commit
  (flush + passthrough) da zarar bytes masu yawa haka sun taru, ba tare da la’akari da timer ba.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — matsakaicin adadin sake buɗe stream na upstream
  a bayyane ba tare da client ya sani ba yayin da holdback bai riga ya yi commit ba.

> **Iyakance ƙimar sliding-window ta kowane provider (babu env var):** _fallback_ na
> iyakance ƙimar sliding-window ta kowane provider da aka ɗauko daga FCC yana cikin code
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, an haɗa shi ta hanyar
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) amma ana tura shi da **taswirar tsoho mara komai**
> kuma a halin yanzu **ba shi da env var na operator** — ana kunna shi ne kawai ta hanyar test hook /
> gyaran code. Da gangan ba a jera shi a jadawalin da ke sama ba. Mai iyakance relay na kowane-`(token, IP)`
> wanda _yake_ da maɓallin daidaitawa shi ne `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

---

## 22. Gano Kurakurai

> [!CAUTION]
> Waɗannan sauye-sauyen suna samar da **cikakken fitarwa** kuma suna iya fallasa bayanai masu muhimmanci. **Kada a taɓa kunna su a yanayin samarwa.**

| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(ba a saita ba)_                | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Wani suna na `CURSOR_DEBUG` mai dacewa da tsoffin sigogi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | an kunna (`!== "0"`)             | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Umarnin tabbatar da kayan aiki wanda ke sa composer-2.5 ya riƙa yin kiran kayan aiki yadda ya kamata. Saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

A kashe yake ta tsohuwa — dabarun hasashen sun dace da tsarin rubutun Turanci kuma suna sauya bayanan amsa, don haka amsar da ta fara da irin wannan sashe bisa halas za ta rasa shi.                                              |

## 23. Haɗin GitHub

Ba wa masu amfani damar kai rahoton matsaloli kai tsaye daga Dashboard.

| Sauyi                 | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------- | ----------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`     | Ma'ajiyar lamba a tsarin `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                                               |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`     | GitHub Personal Access Token mai izinin `issues:write`.                                                                                                                              |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(ba a saita ba)_ | tantance matsaloli / mataimakan cloud agent | Babban token na samun damar GitHub da ake amfani da shi a matsayin madadin `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, kuma mataimakan cloud agent da ke cikin `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` suna amfani da shi. |

## Yanayin Turawa

Don jagorar SRE na backend ɗin relay (halayen ts/bifrost/auto, wurin sanya 9router da CLIProxyAPI, da dabarar madadin aiki mai yawa), duba [Dabarar Backend na Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Ƙaramin Yanayin Ci Gaba na Cikin Gida

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Yanayin Samarwa na Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Muhalli Mara Haɗin Intanet / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS tare da Reverse Proxy (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

## 24. Skills Sandbox (v3.8.0+)

Iyakoki da saitunan tsaro da ake amfani da su lokacin da tsarin Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) ke gudanar da ayyukan atomatik da masu amfani suka ayyana a cikin keɓantaccen muhalli.

| Sauyi                             | Tsoho                                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                    | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Iyakar lokacin agogon zahiri ga kowace gudanarwar lambar skill a sandbox. Iyaka ce tsayayyiya; duk abin da ya wuce ana kashe shi.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(yana komawa ga `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Iyakar lokacin tsara gudanarwar skill a babban mataki. Saita shi sama da `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` don ba da damar tsarin aiki mai matakai da yawa.                                                                                                                                          |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin adadin bytes da skill zai iya karantawa daga kowane fayil guda ɗaya da ke cikin sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Matsakaicin adadin bytes da ake ɗauka daga kowace amsar HTTP guda ɗaya a cikin skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tsayayyar iyaka kan adadin haruffan stdout/stderr da ake dawo da su daga kiran sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Saita `1`/`true` don ba da damar haɗin cibiyar sadarwa mai fita daga cikin sandbox. A tsohuwa yana kasancewa **keɓantacce** saboda tsaro.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(babu komai)_                                 | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Jerin hotunan container da aka yarda da su don gudanarwa a sandbox, waɗanda aka raba da waƙafi. Idan babu komai, tsohon ginannen zaɓi kawai ake amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                 |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(tsohon ginannen zaɓi)_                       | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Hoton container da ake amfani da shi lokacin ƙirƙirar sandbox mai amfani da Docker. Sauya shi don kayyade hoton tushe na musamman da aka ƙarfafa tsaronsa.                                                                                                                                     |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                         | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Runtime na container don keɓance skills: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` yana zaɓar runtime mafi dacewa da aka girka bisa ga OS na host (Apple Container/OrbStack a macOS, WSL Container a Windows, Podman a Linux), sannan ya koma Docker idan babu. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Kunna `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` yana buɗe hanyar fita daga kowace irin lambar skill. Haɗa shi da `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` da ƙaƙƙarfan manufar `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy a turawar da ake amfani da ita tare.

## 25. Ƙayyadaddun Amfani na Masu Bayarwa, Tunnels, Ajiyar Bayanai & Sauran Abubuwan Lokacin Gudanarwa

Wuraren samun bayanai na ƙayyadaddun amfani na masu bayarwa, tunnels na hanyar sadarwa (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), rukunin fitarwa na 1Proxy, ajiyar bayanan rumbun bayanai da kuma ƙananan sauye-sauye na kowane fasali waɗanda matakin executor ko scripts ke amfani da su.

| Variable                                       | Default                                                                     | Source File                                                               | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Zaren haɗin Redis don backend na rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(production host)_                                                         | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Sauya host ɗin da ake amfani da shi don ɗauko ƙayyadaddun amfani na tsarin coding na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | an samo daga host                                                           | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Sauya cikakken URL na ƙayyadaddun amfani na Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie na zaman console don mashigar ƙayyadaddun amfani ta keɓaɓɓen Token Plan na Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (maɓallin inference API ba zai iya karanta shi ba). Kwafi dukkan request header na `Cookie` — yana ɗauke da `login_qwencloud_ticket` — daga kowane kiran `api.json` zuwa `cs-data.qwencloud.com` a home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Yana da sirri kuma ya keɓanta ga zaman; an fi son filin Dashboard na `qwenCloudCookie` na kowace haɗi.                                                                                                     |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Sauyin `sec_token` na hannu don mashigar console ta Token Plan. Yana da sirri; idan ba a saita shi ba, fetcher zai samo shi daga HTML na dashboard ta amfani da cookie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Sauya host na mashiga don fetcher na ƙayyadaddun amfani na keɓaɓɓen Token Plan (misali, `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` don console na Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL na dashboard da ake amfani da shi don samo `sec_token` daga HTML da aka shiga cikinsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Sauya hanyar front-end ta console don ɗauko ƙayyadaddun amfani na vision/media na matakin kyauta na Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Sauya ta musamman ga hanyar gaban-fage ta console don ɗauko ƙason multimodal na matakin kyauta na Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_AUDIO_FE_PATH`              | `/costing-balance/free-quota-audio`                                         | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Sauya ta musamman ga hanyar gaban-fage ta console don ɗauko ƙason sauti na matakin kyauta na Alibaba Model Studio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_ALLOWLIST_PATH`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierAllowlist.ts`                           | Hanya ta zaɓi zuwa fayil ɗin JSON na cikin gida don maye gurbin jerin samfuran rubutu da aka amince da su na matakin kyauta na Alibaba wanda yake cikin tsarin. Idan bai samu ba, zai koma `$DATA_DIR/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`, sannan `config/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CONTEXT_RESERVE_TOKENS`                       | `1024`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Token da aka keɓe don fitowar kammalawa yayin ƙididdige iyakokin prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CONTEXT_KEEP_LATEST_IMAGES`                   | `2`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Adadin sabbin hotunan inline da za a riƙe yayin cire tsofaffi domin su dace da taga mahallin (#8560).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MODEL_ALIAS_COMPAT_ENABLED`                   | an kunna                                                                    | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                                              | Kunna ko kashe matakin dacewa da tsoffin laƙabin model da tsofaffin abokan hulɗa ke amfani da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_EMERGENCY_FALLBACK`                 | an kunna                                                                    | `open-sse/services/emergencyFallback.ts`                                  | Saita `false` (ko `0`) don kashe madadin gaggawa na ƙarewar kasafi wanda ke sake tura buƙatun da suka gaza zuwa model ɗin kyauta na `nvidia`/`openai/gpt-oss-120b`. Tsarin fifiko mai aiki shi ne sauya ta Feature Flags DB > env var > ƙimar asali; idan babu shi, sabis ɗin zai koma ga ɗanyen ƙimar env.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| `COMMAND_CODE_VERSION`                         | `0.33.2`                                                                    | `open-sse/executors/commandCode.ts`                                       | Ƙimar da ake aikawa a matsayin header na `x-command-code-version` zuwa uwar-garken Command Code.
