| `COMMANDCODE_API_URL`                          | `https://api.commandcode.ai`                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/command-code.ts`                                 | Tushen URL na uwar-garken amfani/ƙaso na Command Code wanda telemetry na mai ɗauko ƙason smartphone ke amfani da shi. Yi sauya ta musamman don API na Command Code mai masaukin kansa ko na daban.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MITM_LOCAL_PORT`                              | `443`                                                                       | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Tashar haɗin gida don wakilin gyaran kuskure na MITM.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MITM_DISABLE_TLS_VERIFY`                      | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`                                                     | Saita `1` don kashe tabbatar da TLS na uwar-garke ta sama (don haɓakawa kawai).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MITM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | `60000`                                                                     | `src/mitm/socketTimeouts.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                       | Wa'adin ƙarewar soket mara aiki (ms) don haɗe-haɗen da ake wakilta; ana katse soket-soket marasa aiki da suka wuce wannan lokaci don kauce wa barin ramukan haɗi da ba su gama buɗewa ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MITM_VERBOSE`                                 | `1`                                                                         | `src/mitm/server.cjs`, `src/mitm/_internal/bypass.cjs`                    | Yawan bayanan rajistar shawarar hanyar zirga-zirga: `0` yana hana fitar da bayanai, ƙimomi mafi girma suna rajistar ƙarin shawarwarin tsallakewa/hanyar zirga-zirga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Tantancewa/iyakance-ƙima/kariyar-allura/jerin-izini koyaushe suna fara aiki a hanyar Next. Amsoshi suna ɗauke da `X-Routing-Backend` / `X-Routing-Fallback` / `X-Routing-Fallback-Reason`. |
| `RELAY_ROUTING_BACKEND`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Wani sunan da ake karɓa don `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` (ƙimomin `ts \| bifrost \| auto` iri ɗaya). `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` yana da fifiko idan an saita dukansu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS`        | `5000`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrostCooldown.ts`                | Lokacin jira (ms) bayan tsallakewar Bifrost sidecar ta gaza a yanayin `auto` kafin relay ya sake gwada sidecar ɗin; yana aika kai tsaye zuwa hanyar TS yayin da lokacin jiran ke gudana, sannan ya sake gwadawa. `0` yana kashe wannan. Yana aiki ne kawai idan `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND=auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`                           | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Hanyar zuwa takardar shaidar TLS ta PEM don gudanar da `omniroute serve` ta HTTPS (daidai da `--tls-cert`). Dole ne a haɗa shi da `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`; sannan sabar mai zaman kanta za ta ƙare TLS a kan listener ɗin nan (`wss://` yana aiki ba tare da canji ba). Ba a saita ba → HTTP na yau da kullum. Bayar da cert ko key ɗaya kawai, ko hanyar da ba za a iya karantawa ba, yana rubuta gargaɗi kuma ya ci gaba da amfani da HTTP.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`                            | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Hanyar zuwa maɓallin sirri na TLS na PEM don HTTPS na `omniroute serve` (daidai da `--tls-key`). Dole ne a haɗa shi da `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Duba `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Mai Tsara Dumama na Claude

Dumama mai amfani da cron ga haɗin OAuth na Anthropic da aka amince da su, domin a buɗe tagar iyakar amfani ta awa 5 ta hanyar ƙaramin buƙata da aka tsara maimakon buƙata ta farko ta ainihi (#8848). Mai tsara ba ya aiki sai idan `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` yana da ƙimar gaskiya **kuma** an yi wa haɗin alama a cikin `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; jerin haɗi mara komai yana nufin ba za a dumama komai ba ko da an kunna canjin muhalli.

| Canji                          | Tsoho                       | Fayil ɗin Tushe              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(ba a saita ba → a kashe)_ | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Babban maɓallin kunna mai tsara dumama. Yana karɓar `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (ba tare da la'akari da girman haruffa ba, bayan cire sarari). Duk wata ƙima dabam, ko rashin saitawa, yana barin mai tsara a kashe. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Bayanin cron mai filaye biyar don lokacin gudanar da dumama, wanda ake kimantawa a `America/Los_Angeles` (yankin lokacin sake saiti na Anthropic) ba tare da la'akari da agogon uwar garken ba.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Yawan haɗin da ake dumamawa a lokaci guda a kowane zagaye. Ana iyakance shi zuwa `1`-`10`; ƙimar da ba lamba ba za ta koma `3`.                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Samfurin da ake amfani da shi don buƙatar dumama. A sauya shi kawai idan tsohon ba ya samuwa a tsarin ku; zaɓi samfurin mafi arha wanda har yanzu zai buɗe tagar.                                              |

### Zaman VNC na Shiga ta Burauza da Laƙabin Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC da aka sanya a cikin kwantena wanda ake amfani da shi don tattara bayanan shaidar shiga ta burauza cikin hulɗa (`/api/vnc-session`), tare da tsohon laƙabin `DATA_DIR`. Dukansu na zaɓi ne — saitunan VNC na asali suna nufin hoton `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` da aka haɗa, kuma ana sauya su ne kawai don hoton kwantena na musamman, tashoshin sadarwa, ko daidaita zagayowar rayuwa.

| Sauyi                                 | Tsoho                                                                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alamar hoton Docker don kwantenar shiga ta Chromium+VNC. Gina `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ko a sa wannan ya nuna zuwa wani hoto na musamman.                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Fayil ɗin binary na tsarin gudanar da kwantena da ake amfani da shi wajen ƙaddamar da kwantenar VNC (misali, saita shi zuwa `podman`).                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar VNC/noVNC da aka buɗe a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Tashar Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanyar kundin bayanan martabar Chromium a cikin kwantenar.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin da ke kan na'urar uwar-gida wanda ke adana bayanan martabar shiga ta burauza masu ɗorewa.                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (minti 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar lokacin zaman banza (ms) kafin a cire zaman VNC mara aiki.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (minti 30)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin ƙayyadadden lokaci (ms) na tsawon zaman VNC guda ɗaya.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin adadin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana a lokaci guda.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar lokacin jira (ms) har burauzar da ke cikin kwantena ta zama shirye don CDP.                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iyakar lokaci (ms) don tattara zaman/kukis ɗin da aka kama bayan an kammala shiga.                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin tutocin layin umarni da ake turawa zuwa Chromium da ke cikin kwantena.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓaɓɓiyar hanyar sadarwar Docker da kwantenar shiga ta VNC ke haɗuwa da ita (#12571) maimakon gadar tsoho, don haka kwantenan takwarorinta ba za su iya isa tashar gadar CDP ɗinta ba.                      |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_                                                        | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Tsohon madadi** na `DATA_DIR`, ana dubawa ne kawai bayan ba a saita `DATA_DIR` da `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` ba. Yana gano ma'ajiyar zaman zaren yanar gizo na Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Gwaji & Kayan Aikin E2E

Ana amfani da su ta `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, da `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. A bar duk
ƙimomin da ke ƙasa ba tare da saita su ba a turawar samarwa.

| Sauyi                                  | Tsoho                             | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| -------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Yanayin farawa na E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) don mai gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | yana komawa ga `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kalmar sirrin mai gudanarwa da ake saka wa cikin mahallin Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe binciken lafiyar gida mai maimaitawa yayin gudanar da Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Kashe madauwar binciken lafiyar token na OAuth yayin gwaje-gwaje.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(ba a saita ba)_                 | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Masu samarwa da aka raba da waƙafi waɗanda ba za a haɗa su cikin binciken sabunta token na riga-kafi ba (misali `codex,openai`). Madadin da aka keɓance maimakon kashe binciken lafiyar gaba ɗaya — masu samarwa masu ɗan gajeren TTL suna ci gaba da sabuntawa, yayin da masu samarwa na cascade suke zama masu amsawa kawai. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ƙyale hayaniyar bayanan binciken lafiya a stdout na Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Tsallake ginin samarwa na Next.js kafin Playwright ya fara (ingantawar CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                               | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Tsallake ƙugiyar cire OmniRoute (CI yana amfani da wannan don kiyaye `node_modules` yadda yake).                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                          | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Lokacin jira (ms) don uwar garken ya zama lafiyayye kafin gudanar da gwaje-gwajen tsarin muhalli/ƙa'ida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`    | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL ɗin da kayan gwajin smoke na Electron ke tsammanin ƙa'idar da aka kunsa za ta samar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                           | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jimillar wa'adin lokaci (ms) kafin kayan gwajin smoke su haƙura.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tazarar jira (ms) bayan shafin ya loda.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(ta atomatik)_                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Bayyanannen hanyar zuwa fayil ɗin aiwatarwa na Electron da aka kunsa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Kundin bayanai don gudanar da gwajin smoke na Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don adana kundin adireshin bayanan gwajin smoke bayan an gama gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saita zuwa `1` don watsa rajistan ayyukan Electron zuwa stdout yayin gudanarwar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: sake ƙaddamarwa da kundin adireshin bayanai iri ɗaya sannan a tabbatar cewa ƙaddamarwa ta biyu ta zaɓi direban SQLite na asali.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(binciken PATH)_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Sauya hanyar binary ta Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Tsarin fassarar takardu

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (umarnin `npm run i18n:run`).
Ba a saita dukkan masu canji biyar ɗin ta tsohuwa ba — saita su a cikin `.env` kawai a kan na'urorin
da ya kamata su iya gudanar da mai fassara takardun.

| Mai canji                           | Tsoho             | Fayil ɗin tushe                    | Bayani                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | URL na tushe mai dacewa da OpenAI don backend ɗin fassara.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`     | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Token na Bearer don backend ɗin fassara (ba a taɓa rubuta shi a rajista).              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`       | _(ba a saita ba)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | ID na samfurin, misali `gpt-4o-mini` ko `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `60000`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Iyakar lokacin kowace buƙata a cikin millisekan.                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY` | `4`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` | Buƙatun fassara masu gudana a layi ɗaya yayin aiki a kan fayiloli / yarurruka da yawa. |

## 27. Ciyarwar Radar (Karɓar Baƙuncinta da Kai)

Ƙarin fasali ne na zaɓi wanda tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED ke sarrafawa (a kashe take ta tsohuwa — tutar
fasali ce da ake kunna ko kashewa ta Settings/DB, ba env var ba; duba
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Sauye-sauye huɗu na farko da ke ƙasa zaɓaɓɓun maye-gurbi ne don ciyarwar da ake karɓar baƙuncinta da kai ko aka
ƙirƙira daga reshe, da kuma hanyoyin maɓallin mai tallafawa. Na biyar, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, mahaɗi ne daban marar
ƙimar tsohuwa zuwa keɓaɓɓen rukunin ayyukan mai shi. Duba [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) don cikakken
takardar tsarin da kuma
[jerin matakan kunnawa daga farko zuwa ƙarshe da saitin da ake jagoranta](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Ba a saita janar mai karanta sanarwar Gida/Rubutun Canje-canje ta hanyar sauyin mahalli
ba, kuma bai dogara da tutar fasali ta RADAR_ENABLED ba. Yana karanta URL na ma'ajiyar jama'a
`news.json` da aka bayyana a `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` ta hanyar
GET kawai; ID na sanarwar da aka yi watsi da su suna ci gaba da kasancewa a ma'ajiyar cikin gida ta burauza.

| Sauyi                         | Ƙimar Tsohuwa                                | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Asalin URL da ke haɗa kundin da aka sanya wa hannu daban, tura abokai, tayin masu tallafawa, da ciyarwar Intel. Sauya shi don nuna sabis da ake karɓar baƙuncinsa da kai ko wanda aka ƙirƙira daga reshe. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(makullin tsohuwa da aka kafafe)_           | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Makullin jama'a na Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ko PEM) da ake amfani da shi don tabbatar da sa hannun ciyarwa daga ciyarwar da aka keɓance.                                                                  |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Ni mai ba da gudummawa ne" yake buɗewa (hanyar neman maɓallin mai tallafawa ta GitHub OAuth).                                                                               |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL da maɓallin dashboard na "Tallafa wa aikin" yake buɗewa (shafin biyan kuɗi/tsare-tsare).                                                                                                              |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_                            | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Keɓaɓɓen mahaɗin rukunin ayyuka na mai shi kawai. Ana buƙatar HTTPS sai dai don isarwar SSH ta HTTP loopback; ƙimar da ba a saita ba ko marar inganci ba za ta ƙirƙiri abin kewayawa ba.                  |

---

## Bincike: Sauye-sauyen da Aka Cire / Marasa Amfani

Sauye-sauye masu zuwa sun bayyana a sigogin baya na `.env.example` amma **ba su da wata madogara yayin aiki** a cikin tushen lambar na yanzu. An cire su:

| Sauyi                                                                                                                                                                           | Dalili                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Babu wani fayil na tushe da ke karanta shi. SQLite ne kaɗai direban da ake tallafawa — ba a buƙatar zaɓi.                                                                                           |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Yana cikin tsofaffin takardu/samfuran env amma ba a amfani da shi lokacin aiki. Yana iya dawowa a wata fasalin matakai da yawa a nan gaba.                                                          |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Ba a sanya iyakar wucin gadi ga girman rumbun bayanai ba.                                                                                                        |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ba a ambace shi a lambar tushe ba. Mai yiwuwa an cire tsaftace tsofaffin fayiloli.                                                                                                                  |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba.                                                                                                                                |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ba a yi masa rajista a cikin `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ba (Kimi Coding yana amfani da OAuth, ba fayil ɗin binary na CLI ba).                                                              |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ba a ambace su a ko’ina cikin lambar tushe ba.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | An cire su a v3.8.0. Tsarin lokacin aiki ba ya ƙara karanta waɗannan sauye-sauyen env — bayanan tantancewa suna zuwa daga Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / rumbun bayanai mai ɓoyewa. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Mai aiwatar da Cursor yana amfani da `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (duba §22).                                                                                       |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi a v3.8.0. Kiro yana cikin `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — maɓallin kunna/kashe shi ba ya da wani tasiri.                                                                            |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | An cire shi tare da sauran samfuran maɓallan API na masu samarwa waɗanda ba a amfani da su a v3.8.0.                                                                                                |

### Gyaran Tsoffin Ƙimomi

| Sauyi                     | Tsohuwar Ƙimar `.env.example` | Tsohuwar Ƙimar Lamba | An Gyara                                                             |
| ------------------------- | ----------------------------- | -------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                          | `7`                  | ✅ An cire ƙima mai ruɗarwa; an bayyana `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                          | `7`                  | ✅ An cire ƙima mai ruɗarwa; an bayyana `7` a matsayin tsohuwar ƙima |

### Sake ƙirƙirar saitin OpenCode (kayan aiki na buƙata ta musamman)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` don sake ƙirƙirar `opencode.json`
mai ingantattun ƙimomin `limit.context` da `limit.output` waɗanda aka samo daga matakin
OmniRoute mai gudana. Ba a buƙatar ko ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan don aiki na yau da kullum — script ɗin
kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Maɓalli            | Tsoho                    | Fayil ɗin Tushe                           | Bayani                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Asalin URL na instans ɗin OmniRoute da za a yi wa tambaya don `/v1/models`.                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API don tabbatar da izini a kan endpoint ɗin OmniRoute `/v1/models`. Yana komawa ga `OPENCODE_API_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba.                    |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(ba a saita ba)_        | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Maɓallin API irin na OpenCode (`sk-...`) da ake rubutawa cikin `opencode.json` da aka sake samarwa. Yana komawa ga `OMNIROUTE_KEY` idan ba a saita shi ba. |

### Kayan gwajin compression na offline (kayan aikin ad-hoc)

Ana amfani da shi ta `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, wato CLI na kimanta compression na offline.
Ba a buƙatarsa don aiki na yau da kullum — kayan aikin masu haɓakawa ne kawai.

| Maɓalli                      | Tsoho        | Fayil ɗin Tushe                     | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (fanko) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Shaidun JSON da mai gudanarwa ya bayar ga provider da CLI na compression-eval na offline yake gwadawa (ana sarrafa su da `JSON.parse`). A bar shi ba a saita ba don gwajin dry run. |

### Zaman Browser na VNC

Ana amfani da su ta `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` don saita zaman Chromium marasa nuni masu tushen Docker ga provider na sarrafa browser ta atomatik. Duk na zaɓi ne — an nuna tsoffin ƙimomi a ƙasa.

| Maɓalli                               | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                             | Bayani                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Hanya zuwa binary na Docker da ake amfani da shi don ƙaddamar da container na VNC.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Image na Docker don container na VNC Chromium.                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(flags na ciki)_              | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ƙarin args na CLI na Chromium da ake miƙawa browser da ke cikin container.                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port na VNC a cikin container.                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port na Chrome DevTools Protocol a cikin container.                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin adireshin profile a cikin container.                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kundin adireshi na ɓangaren host don profile na browser masu ɗorewa.                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewa saboda rashin aiki (ms) kafin a tattara zaman VNC.                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin tsawon zaman (ms).                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Matsakaicin zaman VNC da za su iya gudana lokaci guda.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewar jiran browser ya shirya (ms).                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Lokacin ƙarewar tattarawa/tsaftacewa (ms).                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Keɓantacciyar network ta Docker da container yake shiga (#12571), wadda ba ta kan default bridge. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Kundin adireshi don adana zaman thread na Notion na dindindin.                                    |

### Tabbatar da izinin sabis na ciki

| Maɓalli                                 | Tsoho | Bayani                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------- | ----- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –     | Token na inline don tabbatar da izinin sadarwar sabis-zuwa-sabis na management-plane.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –     | Hanya zuwa fayil mai ɗauke da token na sabis na ciki (an fi so a cikin container; yana rinjayar maɓallin inline). |

### Ƙididdigar provider na OpenRouter

| Maɓalli                             | Tsoho     | Bayani                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Saita zuwa `false` don tsallake ɗebo ƙididdigar kowane provider na OpenRouter don wadatar da catalog. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL na cache (ms) don ƙididdigar provider na OpenRouter da aka ɗebo.                                  |

### Haɗin Redis da aka saka a ciki

| Sauƙaƙe                     | Tsoho       | Bayani                                                                          |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adireshin ɗaurewa na sabis ɗin Redis da aka saka a ciki.                        |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Tashar jiragen ruwa ta sabis ɗin Redis da aka saka a ciki.                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Keɓantaccen sauyi na OmniRoute don adireshin ɗaurewar Redis da aka saka a ciki. |

## 24. Ƙarin abubuwan sakin v3.8.50

An gabatar da waɗannan saitunan bayan hoton kwangilar muhalli na baya.

| Canji                                       | Tsoho                      | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                                          | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Matsakaicin lokacin jira don samun gurbin karɓar hira mai nauyi kafin a mayar da `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa; ɗan gajeren jira mai iyaka yana jera yawaitar buƙatun wakilai maimakon mayar da `503` nan take. `0` yana dawo da ƙin karɓa nan take.                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Kasafin bytes da aka jera don jiran karɓa: yana iyakance jimillar bytes na body da aka buffer waɗanda aka ajiye a faɗin process domin jiran kada ya ƙara girman heap (#4380). Jira da ya wuce kasafin yana karɓar `503` da za a iya sake gwadawa nan take.                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon saiti mara tasiri tun daga #10110: an cire layukan karɓa na kowane session don amfani da kasafi guda ɗaya na faɗin process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar tsari; amma ana watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Tsohon saiti mara tasiri tun daga #10110: an cire layukan karɓa na kowane session don amfani da kasafi guda ɗaya na faɗin process. Ana karɓarsa don dacewar tsari; amma ana watsi da shi.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (a kashe)              | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Layukan karɓa na kama-da-wane masu daidaitawa a runtime (#9654): babban maɓalli na adaptive gate na kowane tenant (tsari na 2). Ya bambanta da tsoffin vars na layin kowane connection da ke sama (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, marasa tasiri tun daga #10110). Feature flag na dashboard mai suna iri ɗaya; env var yana rinjayar override na dashboard; yana buƙatar sake kunnawa. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Yana iyakance tsawon lokacin da kiran run-now zai jira job da ke gudana kafin fara run ɗin da aka jera.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | a kunne                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana sabunta yanayin IMS da browser-risk ta hanyar Chrome CDP sessions na kowane account; saita `0` don kashewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | a kunne                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Yana adana Adobe sessions da aka gyara a ƙarƙashin `DATA_DIR`; saita `0` don yanayin memory kawai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Mafi ƙarancin tazara tsakanin aika buƙatun generate na Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Ƙarin lokacin shiru bayan kowace aika buƙata ta Adobe ta uku da ta yi nasara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Yanayin true-headless na debug kawai; Adobe colligo yawanci yana ƙin risk session da aka samu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | ganowa ta atomatik         | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Cikakkiyar hanyar zuwa Chrome executable na zaɓi da executors masu amfani da browser ke amfani da ita idan ganowar platform ta atomatik ba ta wadatar ba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token na BotFather wanda ke kunna webhook mai shigowa kuma yake sanya hannu kan Mini App `initData`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(ba a saita ba)_          | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Sirrin da aka raba wanda aka yi rajista ta `setWebhook` kuma ake tabbatar da shi ta hanyar header na `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` a kowane isar da webhook. Ana buƙatarsa don hanyar webhook; idan ba a saita shi ba, ana ƙin isar da webhook da 503.                                                                                                                     |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Model da ake amfani da shi don amsoshin hirar Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Sauyin tushe URL na Bot API don proxies ko sabar Bot API da aka ɗauki nauyin hosting ɗinta da kai.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Timeout cikin milliseconds don kiran Bot API masu fita.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (a kunne)              | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Saita `0` don tsallake samar da `.tar.gz` tarballs yayin shirya optional ML/browser packs don standalone tree na Electron (har yanzu ana samar da pack directories da `optional-packs.index.json`). Desktop release workflow yana amfani da wannan don rage girman artifact da ake lodawa.                                                                                     |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Tsoffin saitunan gama-gari don headless browser da tool-tunnel mai fita. Ƙimomin Connection da aka saita a Dashboard suna da fifiko.

| Sauyi                                   | Tsoho                          | Fayil ɗin Tushe                                                            | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium don gudanarwa ta npm, systemd da PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Madadin gama-gari don takamaiman hanyar Chrome/Chromium.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Endpoint na CDP na ciki; Docker yana amfani da sidecar a port `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Idan an saita shi, dole ne kowace buƙata zuwa sidecar na proxy ɗin CDP ta aika wannan ƙimar tare a cikin header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` (#13679). Idan babu ƙima, proxy ɗin zai tura buƙatun ba tare da tantancewa ba — a wannan yanayin, keɓewar cibiyar sadarwar Compose `chatgpt-web-codex-net` ce kaɗai ke ba da kariya. Ƙirƙira da `openssl rand -hex 32`. |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | ID na tunnel na OpenAI na gama-gari don zagayen kayan aikin Codex na gida.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Runtime key na tunnel na gama-gari; kada a taɓa fitar da shi cikin logs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ainihin sunan sabon ChatGPT Custom Connector da aka ƙirƙira don gadar MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Keɓaɓɓen kundin adireshi don yanayin browser, broker da tunnel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Idan ƙimar ta kasance `1`, za a ɗauki hotunan binciken matsalar browser a kowane checkpoint.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Hanyar cikakke ta zaɓi zuwa launcher binary mai ɗorewa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(ganowa ta atomatik)_         | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Hanyar cikakke ta zaɓi zuwa Bun runtime binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(ba a saita ba)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Tsohon madadi don `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; sabbin saituna suna amfani da sunan canonical.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

---

## Gadar OmniConductor

Mai karɓar SSE mai aiki na dogon lokaci wanda ke kwafin ayyukan cibiyar OmniConductor zuwa A2A TaskManager na cikin gida (`src/lib/conductor/`). Sai an kunna — gadar tana farawa ne kawai idan an saita `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Na ɓangaren sabar kawai: dole ne alamar cibiyar kada ta taɓa isa burauza.

| Sauyi                          | Tsoho                   | Fayil ɗin Tushe                  | Bayani                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL na asali na cibiyar OmniConductor (misali `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Ba a saita ba = an kashe gada.                                 |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Shaidar cibiyar don rafin SSE — ƙirƙiri takwaran nau'in `spokesperson` a cibiyar (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).                            |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(babu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Shaida don miƙa ayyukan A2A→cibiya masu shigowa (`POST /v1/tasks`); tana komawa amfani da `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` idan ba a saita ta ba. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL na asali na sabis ɗin spokesperson (Faro) da ke bayan wakilin hirar dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).                              |

### Tsarawa mai la'akari da ƙayyadadden kaso

Ana amfani da shi ta `open-sse/services/combo.ts` da `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` don binciken kasafin token kafin buƙata. Sai an kunna — halayyar zaɓin hanya ta tsohuwa ba ta canzawa idan ba a saita shi ba.

| Sauyi                           | Tsoho | Fayil ɗin Tushe              | Bayani                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------- | ----- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING` | `0`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts` | Idan `1`, a tsallake haɗin da kasafin token dinsu na kowane zangon lokaci (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, teburin `provider_quota_state`) ba zai iya ɗaukar kiyasin kuɗin buƙatar kafin aikawa ba. A ci gaba da aiki idan babu kasafin da aka saita. |
